{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/codex/repos/ns006-1398-planeval/.claude/skills/netscript-harness\n\n# NetScript Harness (V3) — Orchestration Skill\n\nThis skill coordinates harness-mode runs. The authoritative harness docs live under `.llm/harness/`;\nthis skill tells you what to load and in what order.\n\nAgent lanes and their model bindings are configuration, not dogma — the single source of truth is\n[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Only two rules are hard:\n**generator-session ≠ evaluator-session**, and the **slice review gate** (no implementation lane\nself-certifies). Everything else is a per-run lane assignment recorded in the run dir's\n`supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- The user says `use harness` or asks for a harnessed run.\n- Selecting archetypes, scope overlays, or gate sets.\n- Tracking run artifacts, commits, or drift.\n- Understanding evaluator protocol (PLAN-EVAL or IMPL-EVAL).\n- Deciding where a lesson or doctrine update should live.\n\n## When Not to Use\n\n- For package/plugin architecture decisions — use `netscript-doctrine`.\n- For JSR readiness audits — use `jsr-audit`.\n- For frontend/framework-specific questions — use `deno-fresh` or the relevant domain skill.\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n| Concept           | Meaning                                                                                 |\n| ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **9-phase model** | Bootstrap → Research → Plan & Design → Plan-Gate → Implement → Gate → Evaluate → Release → Close. |\n| **PLAN-EVAL**     | Conditional pre-implementation pass for complex/decision-heavy work; hard stop when selected. |\n| **IMPL-EVAL**     | Mandatory final pass after implementation unless the owner explicitly waives it.       |\n| **Tiered lanes**  | Agent lanes A–E with named model bindings; single source `workflow/lane-policy.md`.     |\n| **Slice review gate** | Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews each landed slice before the sign-off commit; no lane self-certifies (A1). |\n| **Supervisor identity** | Every run dir carries `supervisor.md` (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes). |\n| **Plan-Gate**     | Checklist (`gates/plan-gate.md`) that PLAN-EVAL enforces.                               |\n| **Archetype**     | Package/plugin shape profile from `archetypes/ARCHETYPE-*.md`.                          |\n| **Scope overlay** | `SCOPE-frontend.md`, `SCOPE-service.md`, `SCOPE-docs.md`.                               |\n| **Seed run**      | Planning-only run shape (`workflow/seed-run.md`): discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified one-shot GitHub filing. Use when the deliverable is a board (epics + issues), not code. |\n| **Run artifact**  | File in `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` that preserves state across sessions.                     |\n| **Debt**          | Recorded in `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`.                                           |\n\nFor a **supervisor run** (two or more capability-scoped phase groups), also read\n`.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md` and `.llm/harness/workflow/escalation.md`, and track the\ngroups in `phase-registry.md`.\n\nFor OpenHands or local-agent handoffs during a run, also read\n`.llm/harness/workflow/agent-handoff.md` and `.agents/skills/openhands-handoff/SKILL.md`.\n\n## Workflow\n\nThe user may still write `profile: package`, `profile: docs`, or similar. In V3 that field is an\nintent hint, not the final profile.\n\n| User hint                  | V3 selection                                                |\n| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `package`                  | identify `ARCHETYPE-1` through `ARCHETYPE-6`                |\n| `plugin`                   | normally `ARCHETYPE-5`, unless sibling packages also change |\n| `frontend`                 | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-frontend.md`              |\n| `service`                  | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-service.md`               |\n| `docs` or `knowledge-base` | `SCOPE-docs.md` plus any described archetypes               |\n\n1. Read `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md`.\n2. If resuming, read `.llm/runs/<run-id>/context-pack.md`.\n3. Identify the target surface and select archetype + overlays.\n4. Read `gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md` and `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n5. Scaffold run artifacts from `templates/`.\n6. Produce `research.md`, then `plan.md` with locked decisions.\n7. Record Design checkpoint in `worklog.md`.\n8. **For complex/decision-heavy work, run PLAN-EVAL in a separate session before implementation;\n   otherwise record a justified `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.**\n9. Implement one commit slice at a time; push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail) and keep\n   `worklog.md`/`context-pack.md` current after each.\n10. Run gates; record results in `worklog.md`.\n11. **Run IMPL-EVAL (separate session) unless the owner explicitly waives it.**\n12. Close: update `context-pack.md`, `arch-debt.md`, and promote lessons if warranted.\n\nWhen the opening diff is docs-only, proactively apply `ci:skip-e2e` to the draft PR and also apply\n`ci:skip-scaffold` when scaffold-static is not applicable. Use `ci:full` as the escape hatch when\nthe docs-only change must exercise the full CI surface. Record the selection in the opening phase\ncomment so the evaluator can verify that the cheap lane is intentional.\n\n## Agent Delegation Contract\n\nLane assignments and model bindings are configuration. The tiered A–E model, the selection rules\n(source slices → Tier D, batch/parallel → Tier C with committed `workflow.js`, research/doc prose →\nTier B), and the named model bindings are defined once in\n[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Do not restate lane\nrouting here — defer to that file. The items below are the parts of the contract that hold as\n**invariants** regardless of which lane implements:\n\n- **Generator ≠ evaluator (hard invariant).** The session that generates a plan or an\n  implementation is never the session that evaluates it. Which model implements is configurable per\n  run and recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`; the session separation is not.\n- **Slice review gate (A1, hard invariant).** After any implementation lane lands a slice and its\n  automated gates pass, the Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews the slice content before the\n  sign-off commit — and the sign-off commit is the supervisor's, not the implementer's. This holds\n  for every implementation tier (B Opus sub-agents, C Workflow-generated slices, D WSL Codex); no\n  lane self-certifies. See `workflow/lane-policy.md` for the rule and `workflow/run-loop.md` for the\n  step placement.\n- **Evaluator route binding.** Select the evaluator route from `workflow/lane-policy.md` — the\n  open-model evaluator lane for a formal PLAN-EVAL/IMPL-EVAL, or the opposite-family route for\n  ordinary review — and record it in the run. The two-failure eval loop is unchanged.\n- **Tier-D mobile-visibility proof.** A Tier-D (WSL Codex) implementation slice is launched only via\n  skills + `.llm/tools/agentic/` (never ad-hoc `wsl.exe`), and only when the run artifacts include\n  the WSL worktree path, concrete Codex thread id, daemon-managed `remote-control` proof, and the\n  follow-up steering command for that same thread. Without those, record the launch as\n  failed/not-attached. Claude helpers (`codex:rescue`, `codex:codex-rescue`, `codex-companion.mjs`,\n  internal `general-purpose` agents) are local tool surfaces, not daemon-attached Codex threads.\n- **Per-slice trackability.** Every implementation slice is independently trackable: branch/worktree\n  identity, agent/thread identity, files touched, tests run, commit hash, push status, and PR\n  comment/status. Each slice commits, pushes, and comments on the draft PR before the next slice is\n  considered complete (the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail).\n- **Green-gate merge bar.** Merge, publish, or release gates require all relevant tests green with\n  required features intact. For catalog-related work, do not delete, skip, de-catalog, or bypass\n  tests unless the evaluator verdict explicitly classifies the test as stale/irrelevant and the PR\n  comment records the rationale.\n- **Blocked-lane handling.** If an evaluator or implementation launch path is blocked, record the\n  missing launch mechanism in `worklog.md`/`drift.md`, then proceed only with an owner-authorized\n  fallback lane recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- **Skipping a selected Plan-Gate** — for complex/decision-heavy work, implementation before\n  PLAN-EVAL `PASS` is a process failure. Small/mechanical work records `PLAN-EVAL: N/A` first.\n- **Self-evaluation** — The evaluator must be a separate session. The generator does not\n  self-certify.\n- **Wrong evaluator surface** — the generator session may never evaluate its own output. For a\n  **local-machine run**, PLAN-EVAL and IMPL-EVAL normally use a fresh native opposite-family\n  session: Claude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work.\n  Use the phase-bound Minimax/DeepSeek OpenRouter preset only for a genuine third opinion or when\n  the native opposite-family route is quota-blocked. If OpenRouter is then limited, use a fresh AGY\n  Gemini 3.6 Flash high session on the Google subscription. OpenHands is reserved for explicitly\n  cloud-driven work. The **supervisor chooses when to trigger**; never auto-dispatch an evaluator\n  from a sub-agent. Record every blocked route, escalation, and requested/observed identity.\n- **Self-certifying a slice** — a green automated gate is not a sign-off. The Tier-A supervisor must\n  substantively review the slice before the sign-off commit, for every implementation lane\n  (`workflow/lane-policy.md` invariant 2). No lane self-certifies.\n- **Treating lanes as dogma** — the \"Claude coordinates / OpenHands evaluates / Codex implements\"\n  fixed-lane assertion is retired. Lanes are configuration; consult `workflow/lane-policy.md` and\n  honor the run's recorded lane assignments/overrides in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n- **False attached-agent claims** — for Tier-D slices, a Claude `codex:*` skill/helper is not a WSL\n  Codex daemon thread. Require daemon status plus thread id before claiming the user can see or steer\n  the subagent from phone/Desktop.\n- **Carried-in plans as ground truth** — Re-baseline against current `main` before locking the plan.\n- **Monolithic commits** — Commit by slice, not by monolith. Each slice has its own gate.\n- **Raw root CLI noise as a verdict** — Package-quality check/lint/fmt evidence must come from the\n  scoped wrappers, not raw root `deno fmt --check` over Markdown, generated targets, or legacy\n  line-ending drift (which is not a package-quality verdict source unless the plan explicitly owns\n  repo-wide formatting). The wrapper invocations and verdict rules live in\n  `.agents/skills/netscript-tools`.\n- **Scoped wrappers as a full quality verdict** — the scoped check/lint/fmt wrappers pass code\n  containing `any` and honor inline `// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any`, so a green wrapper run\n  does NOT prove doctrine/jsr compliance. For any slice touching `packages/**` or `plugins/**`, the\n  Tier-A slice review MUST additionally run `deno task quality:scan` (fails on `any`+casting and\n  host-side hardcoded plugin names — the two classes that reached `main` in #745) and\n  `deno task arch:check` (doctrine fitness), plus the jsr-audit skill for package/plugin waves. A\n  new `// deno-lint-ignore` or `as unknown as` introduced to green a wrapper is a review-blocking\n  finding, not a pass. Reviewing on wrappers alone is the exact hole that let #745 merge.\n\n## Run Artifacts\n\nRun artifacts live under `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` and use templates from `.llm/harness/templates/`.\n\n`<run-id>` is the current branch name with `/` replaced by `-`, followed by `--<suffix>`.\n\n| File                | Purpose                                                     |\n| ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `supervisor.md`     | supervisor identity (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes) — written at run start |\n| `research.md`       | deep findings, re-baseline of carried-in plans              |\n| `plan.md`           | approved scope, archetype, gates, debt implications         |\n| `implement.md`      | generator prompt when needed                                |\n| `worklog.md`        | implementation evidence and gate results                    |\n| `plan-eval.md`      | PLAN-EVAL verdict (separate session, before implementation) |\n| `evaluate.md`       | IMPL-EVAL verdict (separate session, after implementation)  |\n| `context-pack.md`   | resumable summary                                           |\n| `drift.md`          | append-only drift log                                       |\n| `phase-registry.md` | supervisor runs only: phase-group map + live status         |\n\nThere is no `commits.md` — the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail.\nKeep `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` current as part of every slice. Supervisor runs additionally\nkeep `phase-registry.md`, `final-pr-handoff.md`, and an `escalations/` folder; brief each group agent\nwith `templates/agent-briefing.md`.\n\n## `.llm/runs` Path Caveat\n\nSome search/index tools may skip or lag on freshly written `.llm/runs/` run dirs (the same caveat\nthat applied to the old `.llm/tmp/run/` location). Verify run paths with a direct filesystem listing\nwhen needed:\n\n```powershell\ndir /s /b \".llm\\runs\\<id>\" 2>&1\n```\n\n## Resource Aggregation\n\nWhen external docs or examples matter:\n\n1. check `.resources/deps-docs/`,\n2. check `.llm/tmp/docs/`,\n3. fetch official/primary docs with available docs tooling,\n4. save useful extracts to `.llm/tmp/docs/<source>-<topic>.md`,\n5. cite the extract in the run artifact.\n\n## Evaluator Separation\n\nThere are **two** separate-session evaluator passes. Both run on the evaluator lane selected from\n`workflow/lane-policy.md`. The normal local route is a **native opposite-family session**:\nClaude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work. OpenRouter\nis an escalation only for a third opinion or when the native evaluator family is quota-blocked;\nOpenHands is reserved for explicitly cloud-driven work. No lane may self-certify.\n\n**PLAN-EVAL** (before implementation):\n\n- Runs only for genuinely complex or decision-heavy work; quick fixes record `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.\n- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n- Reads `evaluator/plan-protocol.md` + `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n- Reads `research.md`, `plan.md`, and the `## Design` section.\n- Writes `plan-eval.md`.\n- Emits `PASS` or `FAIL_PLAN`.\n- Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate.\n\n**IMPL-EVAL** (final pass, after implementation):\n\n- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n- Generator writes `worklog.md`, `context-pack.md`, and `drift.md`.\n- Evaluator reads `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`, the plan, worklog, context pack, drift, the\n  draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments (the commit trail), selected archetype, overlays, and\n  gate docs.\n- Evaluator writes `evaluate.md` with `PASS`, `FAIL_FIX`, `FAIL_RESCOPE`, or `FAIL_DEBT`.\n- Eval loop limit is two failures before escalation.\n\n## Commit Trail\n\nWhen a run requires commits, the draft PR's commit list + per-slice PR comments **are** the commit\ntrail — there is no `commits.md`. Per slice:\n\n1. commit by implementation slice (message names what the slice proves, not what it contains),\n2. push the branch,\n3. comment on the draft PR with slice scope, commit hash, and gate/test evidence,\n4. update `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` as part of the same slice (a slice whose commit does not\n   touch the run dir is incomplete),\n5. continue to the next slice.\n\nThe draft PR is opened in the same session as the first commit, so its commit list is live and\nreviewable from mobile without cloning or diffing.\n\n## Rescoping\n\nRescope when the real work is materially larger than the approved plan or when the selected\narchetype is wrong. Confirm with the user before expanding scope.\n\nRecord rescope evidence in `drift.md` with severity `significant` or `architectural`.\n\n## Where Lessons Belong\n\n| Content type                                  | Destination                          |\n| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |\n| Generic run mechanics                         | `.llm/harness/workflow/`             |\n| Archetype-specific gates or false-done states | `.llm/harness/archetypes/`           |\n| Stable repeated cross-run lessons             | `.llm/harness/lessons/`              |\n| Package/plugin doctrine navigation            | `.claude/skills/netscript-doctrine/` |\n| Deep domain expertise                         | a focused skill                      |\n| Deferred doctrine violations                  | `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`     |\n\n## Quick Decision Tree\n\n```text\nUser says \"use harness\"\n  -> read workflow/activation.md and workflow/run-loop.md\n  -> resuming? read context-pack.md\n  -> deliverable is a board (epics/issues/milestones from a major feature, refactor, replan,\n     or triage)? read workflow/seed-run.md — drafts only until owner ratification\n  -> deliverable is code for a single scoped change? stay on run-loop.md (a seed run here is\n     ceremony)\n  -> package/plugin? select ARCHETYPE-* and load netscript-doctrine\n  -> frontend/service/docs? apply SCOPE-* overlay\n  -> two or more phase groups? read workflow/supervisor.md + escalation.md, keep phase-registry.md\n  -> read gate matrix + plan-gate.md\n  -> complex/decision-heavy plan or genuine need for adversarial advice? run PLAN-EVAL (separate session); otherwise record PLAN-EVAL: N/A\n  -> supervised? assign lanes per workflow/lane-policy.md (generator != evaluator session)\n  -> slice landed + gates green? Tier-A supervisor reviews before the sign-off commit\n  -> OpenHands/local/cloud handoff? read workflow/agent-handoff.md\n  -> update run artifacts while working\n  -> commit slice? push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail); keep worklog/context-pack current\n  -> discovered violation not fixed? update arch-debt.md\n  -> evaluator is separate session\n```\n\n## Reference Files\n\n| File                                            | Load when                   |\n| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------- |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/activation.md`           | Every harness run           |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/run-loop.md`             | Every harness run           |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md`          | Lane assignment + model bindings |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/doc-audit.md`            | Docs-changeset runs — opposite-family Sol audit + Fable prose polish of Claude-generated docs |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md`           | Multi-group supervisor runs |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/seed-run.md`             | Planning-only board-seeding runs (discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified filing) |\n| `.llm/harness/gates/plan-gate.md`               | Plan-Gate checklist         |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/plan-protocol.md`       | PLAN-EVAL instructions      |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`            | IMPL-EVAL instructions      |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/verdict-definitions.md` | Verdict meanings            |\n| `.llm/harness/gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md`   | Gate selection              |\n| `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md`             | Archetype selection         |\n| `.llm/harness/templates/`                       | Run artifact scaffolding    |\n| `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`                | Debt registry               |\n\n## Checklist\n\n- [ ] `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md` were read.\n- [ ] Run dir has `supervisor.md` (supervisor identity + lane table) written at run start.\n- [ ] Archetype and overlays are selected and justified.\n- [ ] Lane assignments follow `workflow/lane-policy.md`, or the override is recorded in\n      `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n- [ ] Plan-Gate checklist (`gates/plan-gate.md`) was reviewed.\n- [ ] PLAN-EVAL returned `PASS` before implementation when selected, or a justified `PLAN-EVAL: N/A` was recorded first.\n- [ ] Any PLAN-EVAL used the recorded route, or the owner-authorized fallback was recorded.\n- [ ] Tier-D (WSL Codex) slices recorded daemon-managed proof, thread id, worktree, and steering\n      command.\n- [ ] The slice review gate was performed (Tier-A substantive review) before each sign-off commit;\n      no lane self-certified.\n- [ ] Each implementation slice was committed, pushed, and commented on the draft PR.\n- [ ] IMPL-EVAL is a separate session from the generator.\n- [ ] IMPL-EVAL used the recorded evaluator route (open model), or the blocked launch was recorded.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"8f101b13-5500-4793-9417-c73f94472967","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:50:59.103Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/codex/repos/ns006-1398-planeval/.claude/skills/netscript-doctrine\n\n# NetScript Doctrine Skill\n\nThis skill is a navigator. The doctrine remains authoritative; read the relevant doctrine file\nbefore changing or evaluating package/plugin code.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Work touches or describes `packages/` or `plugins/`.\n- Public `@netscript/*` exports need design or review.\n- Package READMEs or JSR readiness need evaluation.\n- Architecture debt entries need creation or closure.\n- Harness plans/evaluations for package or plugin work.\n\n## When Not to Use\n\n- For app, service, frontend, or infrastructure work that does **not** change or evaluate\n  package/plugin surfaces — use the relevant scope overlay or domain skill instead.\n- For JSR publishability audits — use `jsr-audit`.\n- For harness orchestration — use `netscript-harness`.\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n| Concept               | Meaning                                                                                            |\n| --------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| **Doctrine**          | The 10-file architecture specification under `docs/architecture/doctrine/`. Append-only.           |\n| **Archetype**         | One of six package shapes (1 Small Contract … 6 CLI/Tooling). Determines minimum viable structure. |\n| **Axiom**             | A1–A14: foundational assumptions every package must respect.                                       |\n| **Anti-Pattern (AP)** | AP-1..AP-20: common mistakes the evaluator checks for.                                             |\n| **Fitness Gate (F)**  | F-1..F-18: automated checks per archetype.                                                         |\n| **Layering**          | `domain` → `ports` → `application` → `adapters` → `presentation`.                                  |\n| **Debt**              | Recorded violations in `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md` with owner, target, and closing gate.      |\n\n### Archetype Selection\n\nUse `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md` for the run-time decision tree.\n\n| Archetype            | Use                                                  |\n| -------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |\n| 1 Small Contract     | types, schemas, invariants, little runtime           |\n| 2 Integration        | external systems, ports, adapters                    |\n| 3 Runtime/Behavior   | long-running behavior, state, lifecycle, supervision |\n| 4 Public DSL/Builder | fluent builders and definition APIs                  |\n| 5 Plugin Package     | first-party `plugins/*` packages                     |\n| 6 CLI/Tooling        | binaries, commands, scaffold/deploy flows            |\n\nIf two archetypes apply, choose the larger one and fold the smaller concern inside it.\n\n### Axiom Quick Reference\n\n| Axiom | Summary                                      |\n| ----- | -------------------------------------------- |\n| A1    | Public types first.                          |\n| A2    | Simple over easy at published boundaries.    |\n| A3    | 80 percent path is one chained call.         |\n| A4    | Base classes are stub-only contracts.        |\n| A5    | Composition over inheritance.                |\n| A6    | Helpers must be justified.                   |\n| A7    | Web Platform and `@std/*` first.             |\n| A8    | One concern per folder; one reason per file. |\n| A9    | Archetype drives package shape.              |\n| A10   | Composition root over container.             |\n| A11   | Name extension axes before abstraction.      |\n| A12   | Durable workflows are state machines.        |\n| A13   | Crash boundaries are explicit.               |\n| A14   | Tests and gates preserve doctrine.           |\n\n### Layering Quick Reference\n\nSource: doctrine file 05.\n\n- `domain/` imports no package implementation.\n- `ports/` imports `domain/` only.\n- `application/` imports `domain/` and `ports/`; not `adapters/`.\n- `adapters/` imports `domain/`, `ports/`, and external clients.\n- `presentation/` imports `application/` and `domain/`; not `adapters/`.\n- `testing/` imports public surface and testing adapters only.\n\n### Folder Vocabulary\n\nUse doctrine file 05 for definitions. Allowed role names include:\n\n`domain`, `ports`, `application`, `adapters`, `runtime`, `state`, `middleware`, `presets`,\n`registry`, `diagnostics`, `presentation`, `testing`, `internal`, `tests`, and `examples`.\n\nGeneric `utils`, `helpers`, `common`, `lib`, and `interfaces` folders are doctrine findings unless a\nmigration plan and debt entry explicitly cover them.\n\n## Workflow\n\n1. Identify the affected package/plugin surfaces.\n2. Select the smallest archetype that fits (use `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md`).\n3. Read the relevant doctrine file(s) from the table below.\n4. Apply the axiom and anti-pattern checks.\n5. Verify layering and folder vocabulary.\n6. Record any new or deepened debt.\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- **Treating archetype as a folder template** — The archetype is a design constraint, not a\n  checklist of folders to create. Every file must trace back to a concept named in the design\n  checkpoint.\n- **Duplicating doctrine into skills** — Skills point to doctrine files; they do not copy passages.\n  The doctrine is the single source of truth.\n- **Ignoring debt registry** — New violations must be recorded with a closing gate and owner.\n  Unrecorded violations are `FAIL_DEBT`.\n- **Using raw root CLI output as package evidence** — For package/plugin quality gates, check, lint,\n  and formatting evidence should use the scoped wrappers under `.llm/tools/` with explicit roots,\n  `--ext ts,tsx`, and generated/future-wave excludes. Use doctrine file 09 for the full fitness-gate\n  rule.\n\n## Reference Files\n\n| File                                                                   | Load when                                   |\n| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/01-thesis-and-axioms.md`                   | Starting any doctrine-aware run             |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/02-public-surface.md`                      | Exports, `mod.ts`, README, JSR docs         |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/03-base-and-derived-classes.md`            | Classes, inheritance, runners               |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/04-modules-and-helpers.md`                 | Helpers, adapters, `@std/*`, Web Platform   |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/05-folder-structure.md`                    | Folder shape, layering, naming              |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/06-archetypes.md`                          | Archetype selection                         |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/07-composition-and-extension.md`           | Composition root, DI, extension axes        |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/08-runtime-state-failure.md`               | Stateful runtimes, sagas, workers, triggers |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/09-anti-patterns-and-fitness-functions.md` | AP catalog and F gates                      |\n| `docs/architecture/doctrine/10-codebase-verdict-and-handoff.md`        | Current verdicts and remediation priorities |\n| `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md`                                    | Run-time archetype decision tree            |\n| `.llm/harness/gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md`                          | Required gates per archetype                |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/anti-pattern-catalog.md`                       | Evaluator wording for AP findings           |\n| `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`                                       | Persistent architecture debt registry       |\n\n## Checklist\n\n- [ ] The correct archetype is selected and justified.\n- [ ] Relevant doctrine file(s) were read before making changes.\n- [ ] Layering rules are respected (no forbidden imports).\n- [ ] Folder vocabulary uses allowed role names only.\n- [ ] New or deepened debt is recorded in `arch-debt.md`.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"fed4db00-5e15-4e84-a050-b14ad67ab336","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:51:05.094Z","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_SvFZ30Itv2UvgN7653AwWywh","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"body\":\"## Summary\\n\\nA triggered job execution completes successfully and emits a `job.execute` span, but **no record for\\nthat execution is ever published to the durable `job` stream**. The stream contains only the three\\nstartup job-definition snapshots. Any check that expects an execution to appear on the stream — and\\ntherefore any end-to-end trace spanning producer → durable stream → SSE consumer — cannot pass,\\nbecause there is nothing to consume.\\n\\nFound by empirical audit during #1329's runtime gate work. This is a product gap, deliberately not\\nfolded into that PR.\\n\\n## Evidence\\n\\nFull audit: `.llm/runs/release-0.0.5--orchestration/slices/w2-b-1329/join-audit.md`, evidence commit\\n`6c6e5980a`.\\n\\nA fresh execution was triggered against a real generated service:\\n\\n- event/correlation `trg_evt_ea436f98-c6b3-4c14-8138-14610897da80`\\n- execution id `f27ee619-5c8f-426c-ab34-e78b3da5c877`\\n- job `flow-b-callback`, completed in 43 ms\\n- `job.execute` trace/span `02512162b8ecc8b0c75d4fc532656080` / `98f63759e649fccb`\\n\\nStream state around it:\\n\\n- **Before the trigger**, the complete stream was exactly three startup `job` snapshots at committed\\n  offset `0000000000000000_0000000000000926`: `flow-b-callback`, `health-check`,\\n  `workers-plugin-health-check`.\\n- A live subscription was opened **from that committed offset before the trigger** and held for 25\\n  seconds. **It emitted no data.**\\n- **After the trigger**, the complete snapshot still contained the same three records at the same\\n  committed offset. Only the control cursor advanced (`2890128` → `2890130`).\\n\\nJoin result — no stream record contains any of:\\n\\n- the execution id `f27ee619-…`\\n- the correlation `trg_evt_ea436f98-…`\\n- the producer trace id `02512162…`\\n\\nNone of the three snapshots' `traceparent` trace ids equals the execution trace. The only shared\\nvalues are definition-level job id/name, which join `job.execute` to the **job definition**, not to\\nthe execution.\\n\\nThis distinguishes \\\"published but unmatchable\\\" from \\\"never published\\\": the subscription was held\\nopen across the trigger and received nothing.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] A completed job execution results in a record published to the durable job stream, or the\\n      design decision not to publish executions is documented and the surfaces that assume otherwise\\n      are corrected\\n- [ ] If executions are published, the record carries an identifier that joins it to its `job.execute`\\n      span — execution id, correlation, or W3C trace context — so a consumer can correlate without\\n      guessing\\n- [ ] A live subscription opened before a trigger observes the execution record within a bounded time\\n- [ ] A test asserts the join, so a future change that stops publishing executions fails rather than\\n      silently emptying the stream\\n\\n## Impact\\n\\n#1329 acceptance row 6 — \\\"producer → durable stream → SSE consumer in one Aspire trace\\\" — cannot be\\nproven while this holds. #1329's other seven rows are independently proven and its versioned SSE\\nenvelope ships regardless; row 6 is tracked here.\\n\\nNote the envelope work is **not** blocked by this and is not the cause: the consumer, offset commit,\\nreconnect, heartbeat, malformed-frame handling and the extracted-verbatim `EventSource` example all\\nwork against real service traffic. There is simply no execution record on the wire to correlate.\\n\\n## Provenance\\n\\n0.0.5 stable-cut orchestration, W2-B slice, 2026-08-09. Split from #1329 row 6.\\n\\nRefs #1329.\",\"number\":1398,\"title\":\"fix(triggers/streams): job executions are never published to the durable job stream, so no execution trace can span producer → stream → consumer\"}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"1010acd9-8acf-4321-9fc0-e8bf6dda28e8","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:51:25.899Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"{\"body\":\"## Summary\\n\\nA triggered job execution completes successfully and emits a `job.execute` span, but **no record for\\nthat execution is ever published to the durable `job` stream**. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_function_p8va71pd3axu_1","type":"tool_result","content":"0\t# Plan-Gate\n1\t\n2\tThe Plan-Gate is the first of the two harness evaluator passes. It runs at the end of Plan & Design,\n3\t**before any implementation slice is committed**, and is a hard stop: implementation may not begin\n4\tuntil the verdict is `PASS` or the user waives it in writing. PLAN-EVAL runs in a **separate\n5\tsession** using `evaluator/plan-protocol.md`.\n6\t\n7\t## Why this gate exists\n8\t\n9\tPackage-quality Wave 0 skipped Plan & Design even though `run-loop.md` documented a Design\n10\tcheckpoint, because Design was an evidence section inside `worklog.md` rather than a gated\n11\tdeliverable. Decisions that should have been made before implementation were made during it,\n12\tproducing avoidable drift. The Plan-Gate makes Plan & Design a deliverable with its own verdict, so\n13\tthe cheap fix (change the plan) happens before the expensive one (rewrite the code). The\n14\tgold-standard reference is netscript-start PR #95.\n15\t\n16\t## Checklist\n17\t\n18\tA plan PASSES only if every box is checked. Any unchecked box → `FAIL_PLAN`.\n19\t\n20\t- [ ] **Research present and current.** `research.md` exists; any carried-in plan/audit/run is\n21\t      explicitly re-baselined against current `main`.\n22\t- [ ] **Decisions locked.** Architecture decisions are stated with rationale.\n23\t- [ ] **Open-decision sweep.** Every still-open decision is listed and marked \"safe to defer\" or\n24\t      \"must resolve now.\" **If any open decision would force rework when deferred → `FAIL_PLAN`.**\n25\t- [ ] **Commit slices.** Enumerated, ordered, < 30. Each names what it proves, the gate that proves\n26\t      it, and the files it touches.\n27\t- [ ] **Risk register.** Risks listed with mitigations.\n28\t- [ ] **Gate set selected.** Required gates chosen from `gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md` for this\n29\t      surface, plus scope overlays.\n30\t- [ ] **Deferred scope explicit.**\n31\t- [ ] **jsr-audit (package/plugin waves).** The `jsr-audit` skill's publishability rubric has been\n32\t      applied to the PLANNED public surface and slow-type / surface risks are named before slicing.\n33\t      Mark `N/A` with a reason for non-package waves.\n34\t\n35\t## Verdict\n36\t\n37\t- `PASS` — every box checked; implementation may begin.\n38\t- `FAIL_PLAN` — one or more boxes unchecked; return the plan with the specific items and required\n39\t  fixes. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n40\t\n41\t## Phase A reporting\n42\t\n43\tWhere a check has no script yet, PLAN-EVAL reports it as `PASS` with manual evidence or\n44\t`PENDING_SCRIPT` with manual evidence. Absence of a script is not permission to omit the check.\n45\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"d971f64c-1f94-4d64-b80c-50756047eb39","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:51:36.991Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/codex/repos/ns006-1398-planeval/.llm/harness/gates/plan-gate.md","content":"# Plan-Gate\n\nThe Plan-Gate is the first of the two harness evaluator passes. It runs at the end of Plan & Design,\n**before any implementation slice is committed**, and is a hard stop: implementation may not begin\nuntil the verdict is `PASS` or the user waives it in writing. PLAN-EVAL runs in a **separate\nsession** using `evaluator/plan-protocol.md`.\n\n## Why this gate exists\n\nPackage-quality Wave 0 skipped Plan & Design even though `run-loop.md` documented a Design\ncheckpoint, because Design was an evidence section inside `worklog.md` rather than a gated\ndeliverable. Decisions that should have been made before implementation were made during it,\nproducing avoidable drift. The Plan-Gate makes Plan & Design a deliverable with its own verdict, so\nthe cheap fix (change the plan) happens before the expensive one (rewrite the code). The\ngold-standard reference is netscript-start PR #95.\n\n## Checklist\n\nA plan PASSES only if every box is checked. Any unchecked box → `FAIL_PLAN`.\n\n- [ ] **Research present and current.** `research.md` exists; any carried-in plan/audit/run is\n      explicitly re-baselined against current `main`.\n- [ ] **Decisions locked.** Architecture decisions are stated with rationale.\n- [ ] **Open-decision sweep.** Every still-open decision is listed and marked \"safe to defer\" or\n      \"must resolve now.\" **If any open decision would force rework when deferred → `FAIL_PLAN`.**\n- [ ] **Commit slices.** Enumerated, ordered, < 30. Each names what it proves, the gate that proves\n      it, and the files it touches.\n- [ ] **Risk register.** Risks listed with mitigations.\n- [ ] **Gate set selected.** Required gates chosen from `gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md` for this\n      surface, plus scope overlays.\n- [ ] **Deferred scope explicit.**\n- [ ] **jsr-audit (package/plugin waves).** The `jsr-audit` skill's publishability rubric has been\n      applied to the PLANNED public surface and slow-type / surface risks are named before slicing.\n      Mark `N/A` with a reason for non-package waves.\n\n## Verdict\n\n- `PASS` — every box checked; implementation may begin.\n- `FAIL_PLAN` — one or more boxes unchecked; return the plan with the specific items and required\n  fixes. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n\n## Phase A reporting\n\nWhere a check has no script yet, PLAN-EVAL reports it as `PASS` with manual evidence or\n`PENDING_SCRIPT` with manual evidence. Absence of a script is not permission to omit the check.\n","numLines":46,"startLine":0,"totalLines":46}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_function_p8va71pd3axu_2","type":"tool_result","content":"0\t# Verdict Definitions\n1\t\n2\tEvaluator verdicts are stable values. Use exactly one.\n3\t\n4\t## PASS\n5\t\n6\tUse `PASS` when all are true:\n7\t\n8\t- approved scope is complete,\n9\t- required static gates pass or are not applicable,\n10\t- required fitness gates pass, are not applicable, or have valid debt entries,\n11\t- required runtime and consumer gates have evidence,\n12\t- no unrecorded doctrine violation was introduced or deepened,\n13\t- docs and run artifacts are updated enough for resume.\n14\t\n15\t## FAIL_PLAN\n16\t\n17\t`FAIL_PLAN` is emitted only by PLAN-EVAL at the Plan-Gate. The plan is incomplete or unsound (an\n18\tunchecked `gates/plan-gate.md` box). Returns the run to Plan & Design; no implementation slice may\n19\tbe committed. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n20\t\n21\t## FAIL_FIX\n22\t\n23\tUse `FAIL_FIX` when the plan remains valid but the implementation or docs need more work. Examples:\n24\t\n25\t- a required gate fails,\n26\t- evidence is missing,\n27\t- a path/link is wrong,\n28\t- a consumer import was not updated,\n29\t- a false-done state from the profile is present.\n30\t\n31\t## FAIL_RESCOPE\n32\t\n33\tUse `FAIL_RESCOPE` when the approved plan is materially wrong or too small. Examples:\n34\t\n35\t- the task requires a package/API redesign not named in the plan,\n36\t- the selected archetype is wrong,\n37\t- a docs run uncovers code reality that needs a separate implementation plan,\n38\t- external/runtime validation cannot be performed without adding new scope.\n39\t\n40\t## FAIL_DEBT\n41\t\n42\tUse `FAIL_DEBT` when code/docs may otherwise satisfy the plan, but architecture debt handling is\n43\tinvalid. Examples:\n44\t\n45\t- a doctrine violation is introduced without an `arch-debt.md` entry,\n46\t- an entry lacks owner, target, reason, linked plan, or status,\n47\t- new work deepens an existing debt entry without updating it,\n48\t- a run closes a debt entry without evidence that the relevant gate now passes.\n49\t\n50\t`FAIL_DEBT` means the required fix is debt bookkeeping or debt rationale, not necessarily\n51\timplementation.\n52\t\n53\t## Choosing Between Verdicts\n54\t\n55\t| Condition                                                    | Verdict        |\n56\t| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- |\n57\t| Plan incomplete or unsound at the Plan-Gate (PLAN-EVAL only) | `FAIL_PLAN`    |\n58\t| Code/docs incomplete, plan valid                             | `FAIL_FIX`     |\n59\t| Plan/archetype/scope invalid                                 | `FAIL_RESCOPE` |\n60\t| Only debt registry is blocking                               | `FAIL_DEBT`    |\n61\t| All applicable gates and debt rules satisfied                | `PASS`         |\n62\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"86bca990-6f2d-4d68-aefc-f91250198f1c","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:51:37.644Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/codex/repos/ns006-1398-planeval/.llm/harness/evaluator/verdict-definitions.md","content":"# Verdict Definitions\n\nEvaluator verdicts are stable values. Use exactly one.\n\n## PASS\n\nUse `PASS` when all are true:\n\n- approved scope is complete,\n- required static gates pass or are not applicable,\n- required fitness gates pass, are not applicable, or have valid debt entries,\n- required runtime and consumer gates have evidence,\n- no unrecorded doctrine violation was introduced or deepened,\n- docs and run artifacts are updated enough for resume.\n\n## FAIL_PLAN\n\n`FAIL_PLAN` is emitted only by PLAN-EVAL at the Plan-Gate. The plan is incomplete or unsound (an\nunchecked `gates/plan-gate.md` box). Returns the run to Plan & Design; no implementation slice may\nbe committed. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n\n## FAIL_FIX\n\nUse `FAIL_FIX` when the plan remains valid but the implementation or docs need more work. Examples:\n\n- a required gate fails,\n- evidence is missing,\n- a path/link is wrong,\n- a consumer import was not updated,\n- a false-done state from the profile is present.\n\n## FAIL_RESCOPE\n\nUse `FAIL_RESCOPE` when the approved plan is materially wrong or too small. Examples:\n\n- the task requires a package/API redesign not named in the plan,\n- the selected archetype is wrong,\n- a docs run uncovers code reality that needs a separate implementation plan,\n- external/runtime validation cannot be performed without adding new scope.\n\n## FAIL_DEBT\n\nUse `FAIL_DEBT` when code/docs may otherwise satisfy the plan, but architecture debt handling is\ninvalid. Examples:\n\n- a doctrine violation is introduced without an `arch-debt.md` entry,\n- an entry lacks owner, target, reason, linked plan, or status,\n- new work deepens an existing debt entry without updating it,\n- a run closes a debt entry without evidence that the relevant gate now passes.\n\n`FAIL_DEBT` means the required fix is debt bookkeeping or debt rationale, not necessarily\nimplementation.\n\n## Choosing Between Verdicts\n\n| Condition                                                    | Verdict        |\n| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- |\n| Plan incomplete or unsound at the Plan-Gate (PLAN-EVAL only) | `FAIL_PLAN`    |\n| Code/docs incomplete, plan valid                             | `FAIL_FIX`     |\n| Plan/archetype/scope invalid                                 | `FAIL_RESCOPE` |\n| Only debt registry is blocking                               | `FAIL_DEBT`    |\n| All applicable gates and debt rules satisfied                | `PASS`         |\n","numLines":63,"startLine":0,"totalLines":63}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_function_p8va71pd3axu_3","type":"tool_result","content":"0\t# Plan — #1398 publish job executions to the durable job stream\n1\t\n2\tLane: 0.0.6 runtime / public-surface. Control PR #1525. Baseline `origin/main@01aa12b67`.\n3\tResearch: `slices/research-1398.md`. Target branch `fix/1398-publish-job-executions-to-durable-stream`.\n4\t\n5\tThis plan goes to **PLAN-EVAL in a separate session** before any implementation, per\n6\t`supervisor.md`.\n7\t\n8\t## Contract\n9\t\n10\tA completed job execution must produce a record on the durable job stream that a consumer,\n11\tsubscribed **before** the trigger, observes within a bounded time, carrying an identifier that joins\n12\tit to its `job.execute` span. The E2E gate that encodes this already exists and is currently\n13\t**deferred against this issue**:\n14\t\n15\t- `behavior.otel.stream-consumer` and `behavior.otel.traces`, registered at\n16\t  `packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/otel-gates.ts:52-84`, excluded from\n17\t  `scaffold.runtime` / `scaffold.runtime.sqlite` via `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES`\n18\t  (`packages/cli/e2e/suites/scaffold/capability-suites.ts:23-34`), with the deferral reason\n19\t  *\"workers-combined does not install the stream mutation hook\"* asserted by\n20\t  `packages/cli/e2e/tests/presentation/suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`.\n21\t\n22\t**Definition of done for this issue is therefore mechanical, not rhetorical: those two gates come\n23\tout of the deferral list and pass in a real `scaffold.runtime` run.** That is what makes \"no\n24\thand-waved timing\" enforceable — the bounded-time criterion is the gate's own live SSE loop\n25\t(`consume-flow-b-stream.ts:203-230`), not a stopwatch in a PR comment.\n26\t\n27\t## Root cause (verified)\n28\t\n29\tThe hook is installed only by the workers **API service**\n30\t(`plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:67`). The **background** entrypoints that generated projects\n31\tactually run never install it: `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts:89-107`, `:110-122`, `:125-152`.\n32\tGenerated projects run `startCombinedProcess()`\n33\t(`plugins/workers/src/adapter/resources/glue/runtime.stub.ts:21-24`) as the Aspire resource\n34\t`workers-combined` (`plugins/workers/src/aspire/workers-contribution.ts:12,56-61`). Job definitions\n35\tare published from a different site (`plugins/workers/services/src/init.ts:91-111`), which is why\n36\tthe stream contained exactly three startup snapshots and nothing else.\n37\t\n38\tThis is a **wiring gap**, not a missing feature. The producer, the mapper, the hook, and the\n39\texecution record's trace fields all already exist.\n40\t\n41\t## The decision this plan turns on, and the fact that settles it\n42\t\n43\tThe research correctly flagged a sharp edge: every execution-state mutation fires the hook\n44\t(`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/state/execution-state.ts:288,307`), so installing it publishes\n45\t~4 records per execution on the same key — and `executionState.create()`\n46\t(`plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:74-86`) runs **before** the `job.execute` span exists\n47\t(`:91`). Since TC-14 (`select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:122-153`) matches on `correlationId` and then\n48\tasserts the matched record's `headers.traceparent` trace id equals the `job.execute` trace id, a\n49\tnaive \"install the hook, publish everything\" fix can match a record that fails the assertion.\n50\t\n51\tTwo facts I verified directly resolve this:\n52\t\n53\t1. **`job.execute` is a child of the stored dispatch traceparent.**\n54\t   `plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:44` computes\n55\t   `parentContext = tracedContext?.parentContext ?? getParentContextFromHeaders(traceHeaders)` and\n56\t   passes it to `traceJobExecution` at `:108`, which forwards it as `parentContext`\n57\t   (`packages/telemetry/src/instrumentation/worker.ts:300`). So the `job.execute` span **shares its\n58\t   trace id** with the `traceparent` already stored on the execution record\n59\t   (`execution-state.ts:74`).\n60\t2. **The publish span's trace id comes from the ambient OTel context.**\n61\t   `packages/plugin-streams-core/src/telemetry/instrumentation.ts:160` calls `startSpan` with no\n62\t   explicit context, and the header is `formatTraceparent(span.spanContext())` (`:172`).\n63\t\n64\t**Therefore:** if the mutation hook publishes under a context extracted from the execution record's\n65\town stored `traceparent`, then *every* published record — including the pre-span `create()` one —\n66\tcarries a header traceparent whose trace id equals the `job.execute` trace id. TC-14 passes no\n67\tmatter which of the four records the selector matches first.\n68\t\n69\tThat removes the sharp edge without moving `create()` and without restricting publication to\n70\tterminal states.\n71\t\n72\t### Alternatives considered and rejected\n73\t\n74\t| Option | Why rejected |\n75\t| --- | --- |\n76\t| Publish only terminal (`complete`) mutations, relying on ambient context | Passes TC-14 by accident of call placement — the join holds only because `complete()` happens to sit inside the span callback (`:165-170`). Any future refactor that moves the call silently breaks the join with no failing test. It also discards running-state visibility that the stream is for. |\n77\t| Move `executionState.create()` inside the `job.execute` span | Reorders execution-state semantics (the record must exist before the span names its `executionId`, `:88` → `:103`) to serve a telemetry concern. Wrong direction of dependency. |\n78\t| Add `traceparent` to `WorkerExecutionZodSchema` and join on the field | The gate asserts the **header**, not the field (`select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:122-153`). Changing the schema also raises a published-surface version question the research could not settle (\"whether the streams schema carries an explicit version marker\" — unverified). Not needed for the join. |\n79\t\n80\t## Locked decisions\n81\t\n82\t- **D1** — Install the stream mutation hook on the background runtimes in\n83\t  `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts`, mirroring `plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:65-75`. Cover\n84\t  `startWorkerProcess` and `startCombinedProcess`; `startSchedulerProcess` only if it owns execution\n85\t  state. Installation must be idempotent and must not double-install when a process embeds the API\n86\t  service.\n87\t- **D2** — Publish **all** execution mutations (created / updated / deleted), not just terminal ones.\n88\t- **D3** — The hook publishes under an OTel context extracted from the execution record's stored\n89\t  `traceparent`/`tracestate`, so the publish span joins the `job.execute` trace. This is the\n90\t  join mechanism; it is not optional and it is what the new unit test pins.\n91\t- **D4** — **No change to `WorkerExecutionZodSchema`** and no new public export. If implementation\n92\t  shows the join cannot be made without a schema field, that is a stop-and-report, not a decision to\n93\t  take in-slice.\n94\t- **D5** — Remove `behavior.otel.stream-consumer` and `behavior.otel.traces` from\n95\t  `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES` and update the assertion in\n96\t  `suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`. The deferral list is the issue's own acceptance surface; leaving\n97\t  it in place while claiming the fix would be a false-done.\n98\t\n99\t## Slices\n100\t\n101\t**S0 — verify the environment precondition (blocking, do first).**\n102\tRead `packages/cli/src/kernel/templates/aspire/helpers/register/generate-register-background.ts:180-220`\n103\tand confirm `workers-combined` actually receives `DURABLE_STREAMS_URL` or\n104\t`services__streams__http__0` (`stream-url-resolver.ts:154-190`). If it does not, the hook will throw\n105\tat `create-durable-stream.ts:262-272` and the scope is larger than this plan — **stop and report\n106\tbefore writing code**. This is the research's first unverified item and it gates everything after it.\n107\t\n108\t**S1 — hook installation + trace-context join** (D1, D2, D3) with unit tests.\n109\t\n110\t**S2 — un-defer the two OTEL gates** (D5) and make `suite-registry_test.ts` assert the new state.\n111\t\n112\t**S3 — live runtime evidence**: one `deno task e2e:cli run scaffold.runtime --cleanup --format pretty`\n113\trun, with the two previously-deferred gates passing, plus the Aspire trace showing producer →\n114\tdurable stream → SSE consumer in one trace.\n115\t\n116\t## Tests\n117\t\n118\t1. **Unit — the join.** Mutation hook publishes under a stored `traceparent`; assert the publish\n119\t   span's trace id equals that traceparent's trace id. Today's suite\n120\t   (`packages/plugin-workers-core/tests/streams/workers-streams_test.ts:9-104`) has **no traceparent\n121\t   assertion** — this is the gap that let the defect ship.\n122\t2. **Unit — installation.** The background runtime path installs a mutation hook; a test that fails\n123\t   if `startCombinedProcess` stops installing it. **There is currently no such test, which is\n124\t   precisely why `workers-combined` shipped without the hook.**\n125\t3. **Unit — pre-span publication.** A mutation emitted *before* any ambient `job.execute` span still\n126\t   produces a publish span on the stored trace id (pins D3 against the `create()` case).\n127\t4. **E2E** — the two un-deferred gates, live.\n128\t\n129\t## Gates\n130\t\n131\tScoped wrappers over `plugins/workers`, `packages/plugin-workers-core`, `packages/cli`; plus\n132\t`deno task quality:gate` (mandatory — `packages/**`/`plugins/**` slice) and\n133\t`deno task e2e:cli run scaffold.runtime --cleanup --format pretty` for S3. The E2E gate is expensive\n134\tand **serialised across this lane** — it does not run concurrently with any other slice's run.\n135\t\n136\t## Risks\n137\t\n138\t- **R1 (blocking, S0)** — streams env may not reach `workers-combined`. Resolved before code.\n139\t- **R2** — publishing ~4 records per execution against a 256-event / 1 MiB bounded buffer\n140\t  (`producer-contract-v1.ts:47-55`) raises volume on busy workers. Not a correctness risk (drops are\n141\t  metered and settled), but worth a recorded note; #1405's reason taxonomy is what makes any such\n142\t  drop legible, which is why it lands first.\n143\t- **R3** — the two undeclared `@netscript/plugin-streams-core` imports in\n144\t  `packages/plugin-workers-core/deno.json` and `plugins/triggers/deno.json` are real but\n145\t  **out of scope** here. Control PR #1525. Baseline `origin/main@01aa12b67`.\nResearch: `slices/research-1398.md`. Target branch `fix/1398-publish-job-executions-to-durable-stream`.\n\nThis plan goes to **PLAN-EVAL in a separate session** before any implementation, per\n`supervisor.md`.\n\n## Contract\n\nA completed job execution must produce a record on the durable job stream that a consumer,\nsubscribed **before** the trigger, observes within a bounded time, carrying an identifier that joins\nit to its `job.execute` span. The E2E gate that encodes this already exists and is currently\n**deferred against this issue**:\n\n- `behavior.otel.stream-consumer` and `behavior.otel.traces`, registered at\n  `packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/otel-gates.ts:52-84`, excluded from\n  `scaffold.runtime` / `scaffold.runtime.sqlite` via `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES`\n  (`packages/cli/e2e/suites/scaffold/capability-suites.ts:23-34`), with the deferral reason\n  *\"workers-combined does not install the stream mutation hook\"* asserted by\n  `packages/cli/e2e/tests/presentation/suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`.\n\n**Definition of done for this issue is therefore mechanical, not rhetorical: those two gates come\nout of the deferral list and pass in a real `scaffold.runtime` run.** That is what makes \"no\nhand-waved timing\" enforceable — the bounded-time criterion is the gate's own live SSE loop\n(`consume-flow-b-stream.ts:203-230`), not a stopwatch in a PR comment.\n\n## Root cause (verified)\n\nThe hook is installed only by the workers **API service**\n(`plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:67`). The **background** entrypoints that generated projects\nactually run never install it: `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts:89-107`, `:110-122`, `:125-152`.\nGenerated projects run `startCombinedProcess()`\n(`plugins/workers/src/adapter/resources/glue/runtime.stub.ts:21-24`) as the Aspire resource\n`workers-combined` (`plugins/workers/src/aspire/workers-contribution.ts:12,56-61`). Job definitions\nare published from a different site (`plugins/workers/services/src/init.ts:91-111`), which is why\nthe stream contained exactly three startup snapshots and nothing else.\n\nThis is a **wiring gap**, not a missing feature. The producer, the mapper, the hook, and the\nexecution record's trace fields all already exist.\n\n## The decision this plan turns on, and the fact that settles it\n\nThe research correctly flagged a sharp edge: every execution-state mutation fires the hook\n(`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/state/execution-state.ts:288,307`), so installing it publishes\n~4 records per execution on the same key — and `executionState.create()`\n(`plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:74-86`) runs **before** the `job.execute` span exists\n(`:91`). Since TC-14 (`select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:122-153`) matches on `correlationId` and then\nasserts the matched record's `headers.traceparent` trace id equals the `job.execute` trace id, a\nnaive \"install the hook, publish everything\" fix can match a record that fails the assertion.\n\nTwo facts I verified directly resolve this:\n\n1. **`job.execute` is a child of the stored dispatch traceparent.**\n   `plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:44` computes\n   `parentContext = tracedContext?.parentContext ?? getParentContextFromHeaders(traceHeaders)` and\n   passes it to `traceJobExecution` at `:108`, which forwards it as `parentContext`\n   (`packages/telemetry/src/instrumentation/worker.ts:300`). So the `job.execute` span **shares its\n   trace id** with the `traceparent` already stored on the execution record\n   (`execution-state.ts:74`).\n2. **The publish span's trace id comes from the ambient OTel context.**\n   `packages/plugin-streams-core/src/telemetry/instrumentation.ts:160` calls `startSpan` with no\n   explicit context, and the header is `formatTraceparent(span.spanContext())` (`:172`).\n\n**Therefore:** if the mutation hook publishes under a context extracted from the execution record's\nown stored `traceparent`, then *every* published record — including the pre-span `create()` one —\ncarries a header traceparent whose trace id equals the `job.execute` trace id. TC-14 passes no\nmatter which of the four records the selector matches first.\n\nThat removes the sharp edge without moving `create()` and without restricting publication to\nterminal states.\n\n### Alternatives considered and rejected\n\n| Option | Why rejected |\n| --- | --- |\n| Publish only terminal (`complete`) mutations, relying on ambient context | Passes TC-14 by accident of call placement — the join holds only because `complete()` happens to sit inside the span callback (`:165-170`). Any future refactor that moves the call silently breaks the join with no failing test. It also discards running-state visibility that the stream is for. |\n| Move `executionState.create()` inside the `job.execute` span | Reorders execution-state semantics (the record must exist before the span names its `executionId`, `:88` → `:103`) to serve a telemetry concern. Wrong direction of dependency. |\n| Add `traceparent` to `WorkerExecutionZodSchema` and join on the field | The gate asserts the **header**, not the field (`select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:122-153`). Changing the schema also raises a published-surface version question the research could not settle (\"whether the streams schema carries an explicit version marker\" — unverified). Not needed for the join. |\n\n## Locked decisions\n\n- **D1** — Install the stream mutation hook on the background runtimes in\n  `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts`, mirroring `plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:65-75`. Cover\n  `startWorkerProcess` and `startCombinedProcess`; `startSchedulerProcess` only if it owns execution\n  state. Installation must be idempotent and must not double-install when a process embeds the API\n  service.\n- **D2** — Publish **all** execution mutations (created / updated / deleted), not just terminal ones.\n- **D3** — The hook publishes under an OTel context extracted from the execution record's stored\n  `traceparent`/`tracestate`, so the publish span joins the `job.execute` trace. This is the\n  join mechanism; it is not optional and it is what the new unit test pins.\n- **D4** — **No change to `WorkerExecutionZodSchema`** and no new public export. If implementation\n  shows the join cannot be made without a schema field, that is a stop-and-report, not a decision to\n  take in-slice.\n- **D5** — Remove `behavior.otel.stream-consumer` and `behavior.otel.traces` from\n  `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES` and update the assertion in\n  `suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`. The deferral list is the issue's own acceptance surface; leaving\n  it in place while claiming the fix would be a false-done.\n\n## Slices\n\n**S0 — verify the environment precondition (blocking, do first).**\nRead `packages/cli/src/kernel/templates/aspire/helpers/register/generate-register-background.ts:180-220`\nand confirm `workers-combined` actually receives `DURABLE_STREAMS_URL` or\n`services__streams__http__0` (`stream-url-resolver.ts:154-190`). If it does not, the hook will throw\nat `create-durable-stream.ts:262-272` and the scope is larger than this plan — **stop and report\nbefore writing code**. This is the research's first unverified item and it gates everything after it.\n\n**S1 — hook installation + trace-context join** (D1, D2, D3) with unit tests.\n\n**S2 — un-defer the two OTEL gates** (D5) and make `suite-registry_test.ts` assert the new state.\n\n**S3 — live runtime evidence**: one `deno task e2e:cli run scaffold.runtime --cleanup --format pretty`\nrun, with the two previously-deferred gates passing, plus the Aspire trace showing producer →\ndurable stream → SSE consumer in one trace.\n\n## Tests\n\n1. **Unit — the join.** Mutation hook publishes under a stored `traceparent`; assert the publish\n   span's trace id equals that traceparent's trace id. Today's suite\n   (`packages/plugin-workers-core/tests/streams/workers-streams_test.ts:9-104`) has **no traceparent\n   assertion** — this is the gap that let the defect ship.\n2. **Unit — installation.** The background runtime path installs a mutation hook; a test that fails\n   if `startCombinedProcess` stops installing it. **There is currently no such test, which is\n   precisely why `workers-combined` shipped without the hook.**\n3. **Unit — pre-span publication.** A mutation emitted *before* any ambient `job.execute` span still\n   produces a publish span on the stored trace id (pins D3 against the `create()` case).\n4. **E2E** — the two un-deferred gates, live.\n\n## Gates\n\nScoped wrappers over `plugins/workers`, `packages/plugin-workers-core`, `packages/cli`; plus\n`deno task quality:gate` (mandatory — `packages/**`/`plugins/**` slice) and\n`deno task e2e:cli run scaffold.runtime --cleanup --format pretty` for S3. The E2E gate is expensive\nand **serialised across this lane** — it does not run concurrently with any other slice's run.\n\n## Risks\n\n- **R1 (blocking, S0)** — streams env may not reach `workers-combined`. Resolved before code.\n- **R2** — publishing ~4 records per execution against a 256-event / 1 MiB bounded buffer\n  (`producer-contract-v1.ts:47-55`) raises volume on busy workers. Not a correctness risk (drops are\n  metered and settled), but worth a recorded note; #1405's reason taxonomy is what makes any such\n  drop legible, which is why it lands first.\n- **R3** — the two undeclared `@netscript/plugin-streams-core` imports in\n  `packages/plugin-workers-core/deno.json` and `plugins/triggers/deno.json` are real but\n  **out of scope** here. If `publish:dry-run` flags them, they get their own issue rather than\n  being absorbed into this PR.\n\n## Acceptance mapping (#1398)\n\n| Box | Satisfied by | Evidence |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Completed execution results in a published record | D1 + D2 | unit test 2 + S3 live run |\n| Record carries an identifier joining it to `job.execute` | D3 | unit tests 1 and 3 + TC-14 in S3 |\n| Live subscription opened before trigger observes it within bounded time | D5 | `behavior.otel.stream-consumer` passing live (`consume-flow-b-stream.ts:203-230`) |\n| A test asserts the join | tests 1–3 + un-deferred E2E | red-on-regression demonstrated |\n","numLines":157,"startLine":0,"totalLines":157}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_019ff4f4b4707d31ac068641","type":"tool_result","content":"0\t# Research — #1398 job executions never reach the durable job stream\n1\t\n2\tDelegated read-only sub-agent (Claude · Opus 5, `drift.md` D-1). Baseline `origin/main@01aa12b67`.\n3\t**Terminated early on budget** after a narrow discovery pass; the unverified list below is part of\n4\tthe finding, not a gap to paper over. Every claim carries a `path:line` citation from a file the\n5\tsub-agent actually read.\n6\t\n7\t## Root cause — already recorded in-repo, and it is a wiring gap, not a missing feature\n8\t\n9\tThe stream mutation hook is installed by the workers **API service** and by nothing else:\n10\t\n11\t- `plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:67` —\n12\t  `runtime.executionState.setMutationHook(createStreamMutationHook())`, inside a `queueMicrotask`\n13\t  after `serve()`.\n14\t- The **background** entrypoints never call `setMutationHook`:\n15\t  `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts:89-107` (`startWorkerProcess`), `:110-122`\n16\t  (`startSchedulerProcess`), `:125-152` (`startCombinedProcess`). Each builds its own runtime via\n17\t  `createWorkersServiceRuntime()` (`plugins/workers/services/src/service-runtime.ts:80-89`, with\n18\t  `KvExecutionState` at `:84`).\n19\t- Generated projects run `startCombinedProcess()`\n20\t  (`plugins/workers/src/adapter/resources/glue/runtime.stub.ts:21-24`) as the Aspire resource\n21\t  `workers-combined` (`plugins/workers/src/aspire/workers-contribution.ts:12,56-61`).\n22\t\n23\tSo job **executions** run in a process whose execution-state mutations are never forwarded to the\n24\tproducer. Job **definitions** are published from a different place —\n25\t`plugins/workers/services/src/init.ts:91-111` calls `emitJobToStream(...)` per job at startup, which\n26\tis exactly the three startup snapshots the issue observed. Chain:\n27\t`plugins/workers/streams/producer.ts:61-63` → `packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:122-127`\n28\t→ `producer.upsert('job', job)`. Stream path `/workers/executions`, producerId `workers-service`\n29\t(`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:5-6,66-73`).\n30\t\n31\t**The repo already says this.** The two Flow-B OTEL gates are deferred against #1398 with the reason\n32\t*\"workers-combined does not install the stream mutation hook\"* —\n33\t`packages/cli/e2e/suites/scaffold/capability-suites.ts:23-34`, asserted by\n34\t`packages/cli/e2e/tests/presentation/suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`. `behavior.otel.stream-consumer`\n35\tand `behavior.otel.traces` are registered (`otel-gates.ts:52-84`) but excluded from\n36\t`scaffold.runtime` / `scaffold.runtime.sqlite` via `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES`.\n37\t\n38\t## Execution path and the identifiers available at completion\n39\t\n40\tWebhook → triggers service → `TriggerProcessor.process` → `dispatchAction`\n41\t(`packages/plugin-triggers-core/src/runtime/trigger-processor.ts:172`, wired at\n42\t`plugins/triggers/src/runtime/trigger-runtime-processor.ts:80-82`) → `enqueueWorkerJob` (`:318-367`)\n43\tenqueues a `JobMessage` on queue `jobs` with `correlationId = event.id` (`:333`) Both\n117\t  resolve through the workspace today. Whether `deno publish` rejects them is **unverified**.\n118\t- Plugins are Archetype 5 / thin; convention-bearing primitives belong in `-core`\n119\t  (`docs/architecture/doctrine/11-plugin-thinness-and-base-seams.md:16-36`).\n120\t- No `arch-debt.md` entry mentions #1398.\n121\t\n122\t## Existing tests\n123\t\n124\t`packages/plugin-workers-core/tests/streams/workers-streams_test.ts:9-104` covers the mapper and hook\n125\tas units against a mock producer, with **no traceparent assertion**. There is no test that would fail\n126\tif the hook were never installed on the background runtime — which is precisely why this shipped.\n127\t\n128\t## The two decisions the plan must make (these are findings, not proposals)\n129\t\n130\t1. **Volume and key collision.** Every mutation fires the hook (`execution-state.ts:288,307`), so\n131\t   installing it on the worker publishes roughly four records per execution\n132\t   (pending/queued/running/terminal) — each an `upsert` on the **same key**.\n133\t2. **Trace-context placement — the sharp edge.** TC-14 requires the matched record's publish span to\n134\t   be a child of `job.execute`. `complete()` (`job-dispatcher.ts:165`) **is** inside that active\n135\t   context, so its publish inherits the right trace id. Since\n137\t   the selector matches on `correlationId` and both records share it, a naive \"install the hook and\n138\t   publish everything\" fix can produce a record set where the *first* match fails TC-14. Publishing\n139\t   more records is not automatically closer to passing.\n140\t\n141\t`correlationId` reaches the consumer only because `toExecutionStreamEntity` sets `value.correlationId`;\n142\t`createStreamMutationHook` passes no `StreamWriteContextV1` (`producer.ts:112-118`).\n143\t\n144\t## Unverified — do not treat as facts\n145\t\n146\t- Whether `workers-combined` actually receives `services__streams__http__0` at runtime\n147\t  (`packages/cli/src/kernel/templates/aspire/helpers/register/generate-register-background.ts:180-220`\n148\t  was located but **not read**).
