Research — #1398 job executions never reach the durable job stream\n\nDelegated read-only sub-agent (Claude · Opus 5, `drift.md` D-1). Baseline `origin/main@01aa12b67`.\n**Terminated early on budget** after a narrow discovery pass; the unverified list below is part of\nthe finding, not a gap to paper over. Every claim carries a `path:line` citation from a file the\nsub-agent actually read.\n\n## Root cause — already recorded in-repo, and it is a wiring gap, not a missing feature\n\nThe stream mutation hook is installed by the workers **API service** and by nothing else:\n\n- `plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:67` —\n  `runtime.executionState.setMutationHook(createStreamMutationHook())`, inside a `queueMicrotask`\n  after `serve()`.\n- The **background** entrypoints never call `setMutationHook`:\n  `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts:89-107` (`startWorkerProcess`), `:110-122`\n  (`startSchedulerProcess`), `:125-152` (`startCombinedProcess`). Each builds its own runtime via\n  `createWorkersServiceRuntime()` (`plugins/workers/services/src/service-runtime.ts:80-89`, with\n  `KvExecutionState` at `:84`).\n- Generated projects run `startCombinedProcess()`\n  (`plugins/workers/src/adapter/resources/glue/runtime.stub.ts:21-24`) as the Aspire resource\n  `workers-combined` (`plugins/workers/src/aspire/workers-contribution.ts:12,56-61`).\n\nSo job **executions** run in a process whose execution-state mutations are never forwarded to the\nproducer. Job **definitions** are published from a different place —\n`plugins/workers/services/src/init.ts:91-111` calls `emitJobToStream(...)` per job at startup, which\nis exactly the three startup snapshots the issue observed. Chain:\n`plugins/workers/streams/producer.ts:61-63` → `packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:122-127`\n→ `producer.upsert('job', job)`. Stream path `/workers/executions`, producerId `workers-service`\n(`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:5-6,66-73`).\n\n**The repo already says this.** The two Flow-B OTEL gates are deferred against #1398 with the reason\n*\"workers-combined does not install the stream mutation hook\"* —\n`packages/cli/e2e/suites/scaffold/capability-suites.ts:23-34`, asserted by\n`packages/cli/e2e/tests/presentation/suite-registry_test.ts:204-215`. `behavior.otel.stream-consumer`\nand `behavior.otel.traces` are registered (`otel-gates.ts:52-84`) but excluded from\n`scaffold.runtime` / `scaffold.runtime.sqlite` via `SCAFFOLD_RUNTIME_DEFERRED_GATES`.\n\n## Execution path and the identifiers available at completion\n\nWebhook → triggers service → `TriggerProcessor.process` → `dispatchAction`\n(`packages/plugin-triggers-core/src/runtime/trigger-processor.ts:172`, wired at\n`plugins/triggers/src/runtime/trigger-runtime-processor.ts:80-82`) → `enqueueWorkerJob` (`:318-367`)\nenqueues a `JobMessage` on queue `jobs` with `correlationId = event.id` (`:333`) and\n`traceparent`/`tracestate` from `traceJobDispatch` (`:348-354`).\n\nWorker consumption — `plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:35-175`:\n\n| Step | Line | Note |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `executionState.create({… correlationId, traceparent, tracestate})` | `:74-83` | **before** the `job.execute` span exists |\n| `traceJobExecution` creates the `job.execute` span | `:91` | |\n| `executionState.start` | `:131` | inside the span context |\n| `executionState.complete(executionId, {status, exitCode, result, error})` | `:165-170` | inside the span context |\n\n`traceJobExecution` → `withSpan` (`packages/telemetry/src/instrumentation/worker.ts:295-323`;\n`packages/telemetry/src/application/span.ts:32-62`) uses `context.with(trace.setSpan(...))`, so any\nspan started **during** the callback inherits the `job.execute` trace id.\n\nThe stored record already carries the join identifiers:\n`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/state/execution-state.ts:35-78` (`traceparent` `:74`, `tracestate`\n`:76`), with mutations emitted at `:288` (delete) and `:307` (`#save`, created/updated).\n\n## The producer is already reachable — nothing needs threading\n\n`getWorkersStreamProducer()` is a module-level singleton\n(`plugins/workers/streams/producer.ts:28-44`), so the worker process can publish without new\nplumbing. Public API: `createDurableStream`\n(`packages/plugin-streams-core/src/application/create-durable-stream.ts:28-49` options, `:124-146`\n`upsert`, `:148-170` `delete`, `:171-186` `flush/stop/close`, `:295-320` singleton-per-streamPath\nwith a fingerprint-mismatch throw). `upsert` returns `StreamWriteReceiptV1`\n(`producer-contract-v1.ts:97-105`); bounded buffer 256 events / 1 MiB (`:47-55`).\n\n**Environment precondition:** `getStreamsUrl()` requires `DURABLE_STREAMS_URL` or\n`services__streams__http__0` (`packages/plugin-streams-core/src/application/stream-url-resolver.ts:154-190`);\nmissing → throw at `create-durable-stream.ts:262-272`. Whether `workers-combined` actually receives\nthat env is **unverified** — see below.\n\n## Record shape and how `traceparent` is actually carried\n\n`traceparent` is **not** on the stream record. It is a **header**, generated by the producer's own\n`stream.publish` span: `create-durable-stream.ts:222-242` →\n`packages/plugin-streams-core/src/telemetry/instrumentation.ts:159-177`\n(`headers.traceparent = formatTraceparent(span.spanContext())`). `correlationId` defaults to the\nentity key when no `StreamWriteContextV1` is passed (`create-durable-stream.ts:229-230`). The\nenvelope contract requires `operation`, `correlationId`, `traceparent`\n(`packages/plugin-streams-core/src/domain/sse-contract-v1.ts:34-56`).\n\nThe `execution` entity schema **omits** `traceparent`/`tracestate`\n(`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/schema.ts`, `WorkerExecutionZodSchema`), and the mapper\ndrops them (`packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:88-106`).\n\n**This matters more than it looks.** Because the header trace id comes from the publish span, the\njoin to `job.execute` is satisfied by *where the publish happens in the trace context*, not by\ncopying a field. That is what the E2E gate asserts.\n\n## The gate that defines \"done\"\n\n`packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/consume-flow-b-stream.ts:52-107` reads the\n`job.execute` correlation + trace id from the dashboard, then selects; offset/live-SSE loop at\n`:203-230`. The selector `select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:68-115` matches on `value.correlationId`\nelse `headers.correlationId`, and `:122-153` (**TC-14**) requires the matched record's\n`headers.traceparent` trace id to equal the `job.execute` trace id.\n\nConsumer surface: `bindStreamEventSourceV1`, `createStreamSseReplayStateV1`,\n`parseStreamSseEventV1` (`packages/plugin-streams-core/src/sse/mod.ts:30-34`); offsets are opaque\n(`sse-contract-v1.ts:25,62-72`).\n\n## Doctrine and dependency edges\n\n- `plugins/workers/deno.json` **already declares** `@netscript/plugin-streams-core`, and the manifest\n  declares `.withDependencies({ streams: streamsPlugin })` (`plugins/workers/src/public/mod.ts:61`).\n  **A workers-side fix needs no new dependency edge.**\n- Two undeclared-import findings, incidental to this issue but real:\n  `packages/plugin-workers-core/deno.json` does not list `@netscript/plugin-streams-core` while\n  `src/streams/producer.ts:1` and `src/streams/schema.ts:1-2` import it; same for\n  `plugins/triggers/deno.json` vs `plugins/triggers/streams/{producer,factory,schema}.ts`. Both\n  resolve through the workspace today. Whether `deno publish` rejects them is **unverified**.\n- Plugins are Archetype 5 / thin; convention-bearing primitives belong in `-core`\n  (`docs/architecture/doctrine/11-plugin-thinness-and-base-seams.md:16-36`).\n- No `arch-debt.md` entry mentions #1398.\n\n## Existing tests\n\n`packages/plugin-workers-core/tests/streams/workers-streams_test.ts:9-104` covers the mapper and hook\nas units against a mock producer, with **no traceparent assertion**. There is no test that would fail\nif the hook were never installed on the background runtime — which is precisely why this shipped.\n\n## The two decisions the plan must make (these are findings, not proposals)\n\n1. **Volume and key collision.** Every mutation fires the hook (`execution-state.ts:288,307`), so\n   installing it on the worker publishes roughly four records per execution\n   (pending/queued/running/terminal) — each an `upsert` on the **same key**.\n2. **Trace-context placement — the sharp edge.** TC-14 requires the matched record's publish span to\n   be a child of `job.execute`. `complete()` (`job-dispatcher.ts:165`) **is** inside that active\n   context, so its publish inherits the right trace id. But `create()` (`:74`) runs **before**\n   `traceJobExecution` (`:91`), so a record published from it carries a **different** trace id. Since\n   the selector matches on `correlationId` and both records share it, a naive \"install the hook and\n   publish everything\" fix can produce a record set where the *first* match fails TC-14. Publishing\n   more records is not automatically closer to passing.\n\n`correlationId` reaches the consumer only because `toExecutionStreamEntity` sets `value.correlationId`;\n`createStreamMutationHook` passes no `StreamWriteContextV1` (`producer.ts:112-118`).\n\n## Unverified — do not treat as facts\n\n- Whether `workers-combined` actually receives `services__streams__http__0` at runtime\n  (`packages/cli/src/kernel/templates/aspire/helpers/register/generate-register-background.ts:180-220`\n  was located but **not read**). If it does not, the fix is larger than a hook installation.\n- Whether `plugins/workers/bin/{combined,worker,scheduler}.ts` exist as thin wrappers — only\n  `bin/runtime.ts` was read.\n- Whether `deno publish` fails on the undeclared `plugin-streams-core` imports above.\n- The triggers-side execution publication (`plugins/triggers/streams/server.ts`) and whether its hook\n  is installed.\n- `packages/plugin-workers-core/src/domain/job-spec.ts:113-142` (\"event published when a job\n  execution completes\") — not read.\n- Workers SSE `subscribe.ts` (KV-watch, distinct from the durable stream) — not read.\n- `validate-flow-b-traces.ts` contents — not read.\n- Whether the streams schema carries an explicit version marker (bears on whether adding\n  `traceparent` to the entity implies a version bump).\n\n## Handoff — files to open in order\n\n1. `plugins/workers/bin/runtime.ts:89-152` — the missing `setMutationHook` call site.\n2. `plugins/workers/services/src/main.ts:65-75` — the correct wiring to mirror.\n3. `plugins/workers/worker/job-dispatcher.ts:74-170` — identifiers and the `job.execute` context.\n4. `packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/producer.ts:88-119` — mapper + hook.\n5. `packages/plugin-workers-core/src/streams/schema.ts` — `WorkerExecutionZodSchema` field set.\n6. `packages/plugin-streams-core/src/application/create-durable-stream.ts:124-146,222-242` — publish\n   and header semantics.\n7. `packages/cli/e2e/src/application/gates/scaffold/select-flow-b-stream-change.ts:122-153` — TC-14.\n8. `packages/cli/e2e/suites/scaffold/capability-suites.ts:23-34` +\n   `packages/cli/e2e/tests/presentation/suite-registry_test.ts:204-225` — the deferral to remove.\n9. `packages/cli/src/kernel/templates/aspire/helpers/register/generate-register-background.ts:180-220`\n   — confirm the streams env reaches `workers-combined` (**first unverified item**).\n","numLines":176,"startLine":0,"totalLines":176}}}

Parsing through the canonical\n26\t * `TriggerTypeSchema` is the type-honest narrowing — it performs the same\n27\t * validation that `executionState.create` runs downstream, so behavior is\n28\t * unchanged for the valid values that already succeed.\n29\t */\n30\tfunction normalizeTriggeredBy(triggeredBy: string): TriggerType {\n31\t  return TriggerTypeSchema.parse(triggeredBy);\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\t/** Process a queued job message. */\n35\texport async function processWorkerJob(\n36\t  context: WorkerDispatchContext,\n37\t  message: JobMessage,\n38\t  queueContext?: MessageContext,\n39\t  tracedContext?: TracedMessageContext,\n40\t): Promise<void> {\n41\t  const { jobId, payload, correlationId } = message;\n42\t  const traceHeaders = getTraceHeaders(message, tracedContext);\n43\t  const parentContext = tracedContext?.parentContext ?? getParentContextFromHeaders(traceHeaders);\n44\t  const triggeredBy = normalizeTriggeredBy(message.triggeredBy);\n45\t\n46\t  console.log(`[Worker ${context.workerId}] Processing job '${jobId}' (trigger: ${triggeredBy})`);\n47\t\n48\t  let executionId: string | undefined;\n49\t  let claim: WorkerIdempotencyClaim | undefined;\n50\t\n51\t  try {\n52\t    const jobDef = await context.registry.get(jobId);\n53\t    if (!jobDef) {\n54\t      throw new Error(`Job '${jobId}' not found in registry`);\n55\t    }\n56\t    if (!jobDef.enabled) {\n57\t      throw new Error(`Job '${jobId}' is disabled`);\n58\t    }\n59\t\n60\t    const topic = jobDef.topic ?? message.topic ?? DEFAULT_TOPIC;\n61\t    claim = await context.idempotency.claim({\n62\t      concept: 'job',\n63\t      targetId: jobId,\n64\t      idempotencyKey: message.idempotencyKey,\n65\t      messageId: queueContext?.messageId,\n66\t      payload,\n67\t    });\n68\t    if (!claim.claimed) {\n69\t      recordIdempotentSkip(context, 'job', jobId, claim, tracedContext);\n70\t      return;\n71\t    }\n72\t\n73\t    const execution = await context.executionState.create({\n74\t      jobId,\n75\t      topic,\n76\t      // Narrow the wire `string` `triggeredBy` to the canonical `TriggerType`\n77\t      // via the same schema the execution record is validated against. This is\n78\t      // not a cast: invalid values throw here exactly as they would inside\n79\t      // `executionState.create` -> `ExecutionRecordSchema.parse` downstream.\n80\t      triggeredBy: normalizeTriggeredBy(message.triggeredBy),\n81\t      payload,\n82\t      correlationId,\n83\t      traceparent: traceHeaders['traceparent'],\n84\t      tracestate: traceHeaders['tracestate'],\n85\t    });\n86\t\n87\t    executionId = execution.id;\n88\t    const abortController = new AbortController();\n89\t\n90\t    await traceJobExecution(\n91\t      {\n92\t        job: {\n93\t          id: jobDef.id,\n94\t          name: jobDef.name,\n95\t          entrypoint: jobDef.entrypoint,\n96\t          timeout: jobDef.timeout,\n97\t          maxRetries: jobDef.maxRetries,\n98\t          tags: jobDef.tags,\n99\t          timezone: jobDef.timezone,\n100\t        },\n101\t        execution: {\n102\t          executionId,\n103\t          jobId,\n104\t          triggeredBy: message.triggeredBy,\n105\t          correlationId,\n106\t        },\n107\t        parentContext,\n108\t      },\n109\t      async (span) => {\n110\t        const active: JobExecutionContext = {\n111\t          jobId,\n112\t          topic,\n113\t          executionId: executionId!,\n114\t          startTime: new Date(),\n115\t          abortController,\n116\t          span,\n117\t        };\n118\t        context.activeJobs.set(executionId!, active);\n119\t\n120\t        context.workerSpan?.setAttribute(\n121\t          WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ACTIVE_JOBS,\n122\t          context.activeJobs.size,\n123\t        );\n124\t        recordSharedWorkerMetrics(\n125\t          { activeJobs: context.activeJobs.size },\n126\t          { [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ID]: context.workerId },\n127\t        );\n128\t\n129\t        addJobStepEvent('state_update', { status: 'running' });\n130\t        await context.executionState.start(executionId!);\n131\t\n132\t        const currentTraceCtx = getTraceContext();\n133\t        const subprocessHeaders: Record<string, string> = currentTraceCtx\n134\t          ? {\n135\t            traceparent: currentTraceCtx.traceparent,\n136\t            ...(currentTraceCtx.tracestate ? { tracestate: currentTraceCtx.tracestate } : {}),\n137\t          }\n138\t          : traceHeaders;\n139\t\n140\t        addJobStepEvent('spawn_subprocess');\n141\t        const result = await executeWorkerJob(\n142\t          context,\n143\t          jobDef,\n144\t          payload,\n145\t          abortController.signal,\n146\t          subprocessHeaders,\n147\t          correlationId,\n148\t        );\n149\t\n150\t        addJobStepEvent('state_update', { status: result.success ? 'completed' : 'failed' });\n151\t        if (result.success) {\n152\t          await context.idempotency.markApplied(claim!.key);\n153\t        } else {\n154\t          await context.idempotency.release(claim!.key);\n155\t        }\n156\t        recordSharedWorkerMetrics(\n157\t          {\n158\t            activeJobs: context.activeJobs.size,\n159\t            processedJobs: result.success ? 1 : 0,\n160\t            failedJobs: result.success ? 0 : 1,\n161\t          },\n162\t          { [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ID]: context.workerId },\n163\t        );\n164\t        await context.executionState.complete(executionId!, {\n165\t          status: result.success ? 'completed' : 'failed',\n166\t          exitCode: result.exitCode ?? Parsing through the canonical\n * `TriggerTypeSchema` is the type-honest narrowing — it performs the same\n * validation that `executionState.create` runs downstream, so behavior is\n * unchanged for the valid values that already succeed.\n */\nfunction normalizeTriggeredBy(triggeredBy: string): TriggerType {\n  return TriggerTypeSchema.parse(triggeredBy);\n}\n\n/** Process a queued job message. */\nexport async function processWorkerJob(\n  context: WorkerDispatchContext,\n  message: JobMessage,\n  queueContext?: MessageContext,\n  tracedContext?: TracedMessageContext,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const { jobId, payload, correlationId } = message;\n  const traceHeaders = getTraceHeaders(message, tracedContext);\n  const parentContext = tracedContext?.parentContext ?? getParentContextFromHeaders(traceHeaders);\n  const triggeredBy = normalizeTriggeredBy(message.triggeredBy);\n\n  console.log(`[Worker ${context.workerId}] Processing job '${jobId}' (trigger: ${triggeredBy})`);\n\n  let executionId: string | undefined;\n  let claim: WorkerIdempotencyClaim | undefined;\n\n  try {\n    const jobDef = await context.registry.get(jobId);\n    if (!jobDef) {\n      throw new Error(`Job '${jobId}' not found in registry`);\n    }\n    if (!jobDef.enabled) {\n      throw new Error(`Job '${jobId}' is disabled`);\n    }\n\n    const topic = jobDef.topic ?? message.topic ?? DEFAULT_TOPIC;\n    claim = await context.idempotency.claim({\n      concept: 'job',\n      targetId: jobId,\n      idempotencyKey: message.idempotencyKey,\n      messageId: queueContext?.messageId,\n      payload,\n    });\n    if (!claim.claimed) {\n      recordIdempotentSkip(context, 'job', jobId, claim, tracedContext);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const execution = await context.executionState.create({\n      jobId,\n      topic,\n      // Narrow the wire `string` `triggeredBy` to the canonical `TriggerType`\n      // via the same schema the execution record is validated against. This is\n      // not a cast: invalid values throw here exactly as they would inside\n      // `executionState.create` -> `ExecutionRecordSchema.parse` downstream.\n      triggeredBy: normalizeTriggeredBy(message.triggeredBy),\n      payload,\n      correlationId,\n      traceparent: traceHeaders['traceparent'],\n      tracestate: traceHeaders['tracestate'],\n    });\n\n    executionId = execution.id;\n    const abortController = new AbortController();\n\n    await traceJobExecution(\n      {\n        job: {\n          id: jobDef.id,\n          name: jobDef.name,\n          entrypoint: jobDef.entrypoint,\n          timeout: jobDef.timeout,\n          maxRetries: jobDef.maxRetries,\n          tags: jobDef.tags,\n          timezone: jobDef.timezone,\n        },\n        execution: {\n          executionId,\n          jobId,\n          triggeredBy: message.triggeredBy,\n          correlationId,\n        },\n        parentContext,\n      },\n      async (span) => {\n        const active: JobExecutionContext = {\n          jobId,\n          topic,\n          executionId: executionId!,\n          startTime: new Date(),\n          abortController,\n          span,\n        };\n        context.activeJobs.set(executionId!, active);\n\n        context.workerSpan?.setAttribute(\n          WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ACTIVE_JOBS,\n          context.activeJobs.size,\n        );\n        recordSharedWorkerMetrics(\n          { activeJobs: context.activeJobs.size },\n          { [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ID]: context.workerId },\n        );\n\n        addJobStepEvent('state_update', { status: 'running' });\n        await context.executionState.start(executionId!);\n\n        const currentTraceCtx = getTraceContext();\n        const subprocessHeaders: Record<string, string> = currentTraceCtx\n          ? {\n            traceparent: currentTraceCtx.traceparent,\n            ...(currentTraceCtx.tracestate ? { tracestate: currentTraceCtx.tracestate } : {}),\n          }\n          : traceHeaders;\n\n        addJobStepEvent('spawn_subprocess');\n        const result = await executeWorkerJob(\n          context,\n          jobDef,\n          payload,\n          abortController.signal,\n          subprocessHeaders,\n          correlationId,\n        );\n\n        addJobStepEvent('state_update', { status: result.success ? 'completed' : 'failed' });\n        if (result.success) {\n          await context.idempotency.markApplied(claim!.key);\n        } else {\n          await context.idempotency.release(claim!.key);\n        }\n        recordSharedWorkerMetrics(\n          {\n            activeJobs: context.activeJobs.size,\n            processedJobs: result.success ? 1 : 0,\n            failedJobs: result.success ? 0 : 1,\n          },\n          { [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ID]: context.workerId },\n        );\n        await context.executionState.complete(executionId!, {\n          status: result.success ? 'completed' : 'failed',\n          exitCode: result.exitCode ?? (result.success ? 0 : 1),\n          result: result.result ?? undefined,\n          error: result.error ?? undefined,\n        });\n\n        return result;\n      },\n    );\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (claim?.claimed) {\n      await context.idempotency.release(claim.key);\n    }\n    await recordJobFailure(context, message, executionId, error);\n  } finally {\n    if (executionId) {\n      context.activeJobs.delete(executionId);\n      recordSharedWorkerMetrics(\n        { activeJobs: context.activeJobs.size },\n        { [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ID]: context.workerId },\n      );\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** Process a queued task message. */\nexport async function processWorkerTask(\n  context: WorkerDispatchContext,\n  message: TaskMessage,\n  queueContext?: MessageContext,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const { taskId, payload, correlationId } = message;\n  const triggeredBy = normalizeTriggeredBy(message.triggeredBy);\n  const topic = message.topic ?? DEFAULT_TOPIC;\n  let executionId: string | undefined;","numLines":200,"startLine":0,"totalLines":336}}}

Function that executes the job\n282\t * @returns Job result\n283\t *\n284\t * @example\n285\t * ```ts\n286\t * const result = await traceJobExecution(\n287\t *   { job: jobDef, execution: { executionId, jobId } },\n288\t *   async (span) => {\n289\t *     span.setAttribute('custom', 'value');\n290\t *     return await executeJob();\n291\t *   }\n292\t * );\n293\t * ```\n294\t */\n295\texport async function traceJobExecution<T extends TracedJobResult>(\n296\t  options: JobExecutionOptions,\n297\t  fn: (span: Span) => Promise<T>,\n298\t): Promise<T> {\n299\t  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n300\t  const { job, execution, attributes, parentContext } = options;\n301\t\n302\t  // Build attributes\n303\t  const spanAttributes: Attributes = {\n304\t    ...createJobAttributes(job),\n305\t    [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: execution.executionId,\n306\t    [JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS]: JobStatuses.RUNNING,\n307\t    [NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME]: JobStatuses.RUNNING,\n308\t  };\n309\t\n310\t  if (execution.attempt) {\n311\t    spanAttributes[JobAttributes.JOB_ATTEMPT] = execution.attempt;\n312\t  }\n313\t  if (execution.triggeredBy) {\n314\t    spanAttributes[JobAttributes.JOB_TRIGGER] = execution.triggeredBy;\n315\t  }\n316\t  if (execution.correlationId) {\n317\t    spanAttributes[NetScriptCorrelationAttributes.CORRELATION_ID] = execution.correlationId;\n318\t  }\n319\t  if (attributes) {\n320\t    Object.assign(spanAttributes, attributes);\n321\t  }\n322\t\n323\t  return await withSpan(\n324\t    tracer,\n325\t    SpanNames.JOB_EXECUTE,\n326\t    async (span) => {\n327\t      const startTime = Date.now();\n328\t\n329\t      addSpanEvent(span, 'job.started', {\n330\t        [JobAttributes.JOB_ID]: job.id,\n331\t        [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: execution.executionId,\n332\t      });\n333\t\n334\t      try {\n335\t        const result = await fn(span);\n336\t        const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;\n337\t\n338\t        // Update span with result\n339\t        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS, durationMs);\n340\t        span.setAttribute(\n341\t          JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS,\n342\t          result.success ? JobStatuses.COMPLETED : JobStatuses.FAILED,\n343\t        );\n344\t        span.setAttribute(\n345\t          NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME,\n346\t          result.success ? JobStatuses.COMPLETED : JobStatuses.FAILED,\n347\t        );\n348\t\n349\t        if (result.exitCode !== undefined) {\n350\t          span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_EXIT_CODE, result.exitCode);\n351\t        }\n352\t\n353\t        if (result.success) {\n354\t          addSpanEvent(span, 'job.completed', {\n355\t            [JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS]: durationMs,\n356\t          });\n357\t        } else {\n358\t          addSpanEvent(span, 'job.failed', {\n359\t            [JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS]: durationMs,\n360\t            'error.message': result.error ?? 'Unknown error',\n361\t          });\n362\t        }\n363\t\n364\t        return result;\n365\t      } catch (error) {\n366\t        const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;\n367\t        const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n368\t\n369\t        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS, durationMs);\n370\t        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS, JobStatuses.FAILED);\n371\t        span.setAttribute(NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME, JobStatuses.FAILED);\n372\t\n373\t        addSpanEvent(span, 'job.exception', {\n374\t          'exception.message': errorMessage,\n375\t        });\n376\t\n377\t        throw error;\n378\t      }\n379\t    },\n380\t    {\n381\t      kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n382\t      attributes: spanAttributes,\n383\t      parentContext,\n384\t    },\n385\t  );\n386\t}\n387\t\n388\t/**\n389\t * Create a span for job subprocess spawn.\n390\t *\n391\t * @param job - Job definition\n392\t * @param executionId - Execution identifier\n393\t * @returns Span for the subprocess spawn\n394\t */\n395\texport function createJobSpawnSpan(\n396\t  job: TracedJobDefinition,\n397\t  executionId: string,\n398\t): Span {\n399\t  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n400\t\n401\t  const span = createSpan(tracer, SpanNames.JOB_SPAWN, {\n402\t    kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n403\t    attributes: {\n404\t      ...createJobAttributes(job),\n405\t      [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: executionId,\n406\t    },\n407\t  });\n408\t\n409\t  return span;\n410\t}\n411\t\n412\t// ============================================================================\n413\t// JOB SUBPROCESS ENVIRONMENT\n414\t// ============================================================================\n415\t\n416\t/**\n417\t * Create environment variables for a job subprocess.\n418\t *\n419\t * Includes:\n420\t * - All OTEL_* environment variables from parent process\n421\t * - Trace context for continuing the trace in the subprocess\n422\t *\n423\t * @param additionalEnv - Additional environment variables to include\n424\t * @returns Environment variables for the subprocess\n425\t *\n426\t * @example\n427\t * ```ts\n428\t * const env = createJobSubprocessEnv({\n429\t *   JOB_ID: job.id,\n430\t *   JOB_PAYLOAD: JSON.stringify(payload),\n431\t * });\n432\t *\n433\t * const command = new Deno.Command('deno', {\n434\t *   args: ['run', job.entrypoint],\n435\t *   env,\n436\t * });\n437\t * ```\n438\t */\n439\texport function createJobSubprocessEnv(\n440\t  additionalEnv: Record<string, string> = {},\n441\t): Record<string, string> {\n442\t  return {\n443\t    // Pass through all existing environment (includes OTEL_* from Aspire)\n444\t    ...Deno.env.toObject(),\n445\t    // Pass OTEL environment variables explicitly\n446\t    ...getOtelEnvVars(),\n447\t    // Reduce batch span processor delay to minimize subprocess exit wait time\n448\t    // Default is 5000ms which causes 20+ second delays on process exit\n449\t    OTEL_BSP_SCHEDULE_DELAY: '100',\n450\t    OTEL_BSP_EXPORT_TIMEOUT: '5000',\n451\t    // Add trace context for the subprocess\n452\t    ...createJobTraceEnv(),\n453\t    // Add any additional environment variables\n454\t    ...additionalEnv,\n455\t  };\n456\t}\n457\t\n458\t/**\n459\t * Initialize tracing in a job subprocess.\n460\t *\n461\t * Call this at the start of a job script to continue the trace from the worker.\n462\t *\n463\t * @returns Parent context if available, or undefined\n464\t *\n465\t * @example\n466\t * ```ts\n467\t * // In job script (e.g., jobs/my-job.ts)\n468\t * import { initJobTracing, withSpan, getJobTracer } from '@netscript/telemetry';\n469\t *\n470\t * const parentContext = initJobTracing();\n471\t *\n472\t * // Create spans that are linked to the worker trace\n473\t * await withSpan(getJobTracer(), 'job.main', async (span) => {\n474\t *   span.setAttribute('job.step', 'processing');\n475\t *   // ... job logic\n476\t * }, { parentContext });\n477\t * * @param options - Job execution options\n * @param fn - Function that executes the job\n * @returns Job result\n *\n * @example\n * ```ts\n * const result = await traceJobExecution(\n *   { job: jobDef, execution: { executionId, jobId } },\n *   async (span) => {\n *     span.setAttribute('custom', 'value');\n *     return await executeJob();\n *   }\n * );\n * ```\n */\nexport async function traceJobExecution<T extends TracedJobResult>(\n  options: JobExecutionOptions,\n  fn: (span: Span) => Promise<T>,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n  const { job, execution, attributes, parentContext } = options;\n\n  // Build attributes\n  const spanAttributes: Attributes = {\n    ...createJobAttributes(job),\n    [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: execution.executionId,\n    [JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS]: JobStatuses.RUNNING,\n    [NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME]: JobStatuses.RUNNING,\n  };\n\n  if (execution.attempt) {\n    spanAttributes[JobAttributes.JOB_ATTEMPT] = execution.attempt;\n  }\n  if (execution.triggeredBy) {\n    spanAttributes[JobAttributes.JOB_TRIGGER] = execution.triggeredBy;\n  }\n  if (execution.correlationId) {\n    spanAttributes[NetScriptCorrelationAttributes.CORRELATION_ID] = execution.correlationId;\n  }\n  if (attributes) {\n    Object.assign(spanAttributes, attributes);\n  }\n\n  return await withSpan(\n    tracer,\n    SpanNames.JOB_EXECUTE,\n    async (span) => {\n      const startTime = Date.now();\n\n      addSpanEvent(span, 'job.started', {\n        [JobAttributes.JOB_ID]: job.id,\n        [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: execution.executionId,\n      });\n\n      try {\n        const result = await fn(span);\n        const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;\n\n        // Update span with result\n        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS, durationMs);\n        span.setAttribute(\n          JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS,\n          result.success ? JobStatuses.COMPLETED : JobStatuses.FAILED,\n        );\n        span.setAttribute(\n          NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME,\n          result.success ? JobStatuses.COMPLETED : JobStatuses.FAILED,\n        );\n\n        if (result.exitCode !== undefined) {\n          span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_EXIT_CODE, result.exitCode);\n        }\n\n        if (result.success) {\n          addSpanEvent(span, 'job.completed', {\n            [JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS]: durationMs,\n          });\n        } else {\n          addSpanEvent(span, 'job.failed', {\n            [JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS]: durationMs,\n            'error.message': result.error ?? 'Unknown error',\n          });\n        }\n\n        return result;\n      } catch (error) {\n        const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;\n        const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n\n        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_DURATION_MS, durationMs);\n        span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_STATUS, JobStatuses.FAILED);\n        span.setAttribute(NetScriptAttributeDomains.OUTCOME, JobStatuses.FAILED);\n\n        addSpanEvent(span, 'job.exception', {\n          'exception.message': errorMessage,\n        });\n\n        throw error;\n      }\n    },\n    {\n      kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n      attributes: spanAttributes,\n      parentContext,\n    },\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a span for job subprocess spawn.\n *\n * @param job - Job definition\n * @param executionId - Execution identifier\n * @returns Span for the subprocess spawn\n */\nexport function createJobSpawnSpan(\n  job: TracedJobDefinition,\n  executionId: string,\n): Span {\n  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n\n  const span = createSpan(tracer, SpanNames.JOB_SPAWN, {\n    kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n    attributes: {\n      ...createJobAttributes(job),\n      [ExecutionAttributes.EXECUTION_ID]: executionId,\n    },\n  });\n\n  return span;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// JOB SUBPROCESS ENVIRONMENT\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Create environment variables for a job subprocess.\n *\n * Includes:\n * - All OTEL_* environment variables from parent process\n * - Trace context for continuing the trace in the subprocess\n *\n * @param additionalEnv - Additional environment variables to include\n * @returns Environment variables for the subprocess\n *\n * @example\n * ```ts\n * const env = createJobSubprocessEnv({\n *   JOB_ID: job.id,\n *   JOB_PAYLOAD: JSON.stringify(payload),\n * });\n *\n * const command = new Deno.Command('deno', {\n *   args: ['run', job.entrypoint],\n *   env,\n * });\n * ```\n */\nexport function createJobSubprocessEnv(\n  additionalEnv: Record<string, string> = {},\n): Record<string, string> {\n  return {\n    // Pass through all existing environment (includes OTEL_* from Aspire)\n    ...Deno.env.toObject(),\n    // Pass OTEL environment variables explicitly\n    ...getOtelEnvVars(),\n    // Reduce batch span processor delay to minimize subprocess exit wait time\n    // Default is 5000ms which causes 20+ second delays on process exit\n    OTEL_BSP_SCHEDULE_DELAY: '100',\n    OTEL_BSP_EXPORT_TIMEOUT: '5000',\n    // Add trace context for the subprocess\n    ...createJobTraceEnv(),\n    // Add any additional environment variables\n    ...additionalEnv,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Initialize tracing in a job subprocess.\n *\n * Call this at the start of a job script to continue the trace from the worker.\n *\n * @returns Parent context if available, or undefined\n *\n * @example\n * ```ts\n * // In job script (e.g., jobs/my-job.ts)\n * import { initJobTracing, withSpan, getJobTracer } from '@netscript/telemetry';\n *\n * const parentContext = initJobTracing();\n *\n * // Create spans that are linked to the worker trace\n * await withSpan(getJobTracer(), 'job.main', async (span) => {\n *   span.setAttribute('job.step', 'processing');\n *   // ... job logic\n * }, { parentContext });\n * ```\n */\nexport function initJobTracing(): Context | null {\n  return extractJobTraceContext();\n}\n\n/**\n * Get trace context from the current span for passing to nested operations.\n *\n * @returns Serialized trace context or null\n */\nexport function getJobTraceContext(): SerializedTraceContext | null {\n  return getTraceContext();\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// JOB SCRIPT HELPERS\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Create a traced job main function wrapper.\n *\n * Simplifies tracing in job scripts by handling context extraction and span creation.\n *\n * @param jobId - Job identifier\n * @param fn - Job main function\n * @returns Result of the job function\n *\n * @example\n * ```ts\n * // In job script\n * import { runTracedJob } from '@netscript/telemetry/instrumentation/worker';\n *\n * const result = await runTracedJob('my-job', async (span) => {\n *   span.addEvent('step.started', { step: 'fetch' });\n *   const data = await fetchData();\n *\n *   span.addEvent('step.completed', { step: 'fetch' });\n *   return { data };\n * });\n *\n * // Output result for worker to capture\n * console.log(JSON.stringify(result));\n * ```\n */\nexport async function runTracedJob<T>(\n  jobId: string,\n  fn: (span: Span) => Promise<T>,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n  const parentContext = initJobTracing();\n\n  return await withSpan(\n    tracer,\n    'job.main',\n    async (span) => {\n      span.setAttribute(JobAttributes.JOB_ID, jobId);\n      span.setAttribute('job.subprocess', true);\n\n      addSpanEvent(span, 'job.subprocess.started');\n\n      try {\n        const result = await fn(span);\n        addSpanEvent(span, 'job.subprocess.completed');\n        return result;\n      } catch (error) {\n        const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n        addSpanEvent(span, 'job.subprocess.failed', {\n          'error.message': message,\n        });\n        throw error;\n      }\n    },\n    {\n      kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n      parentContext: parentContext ?? undefined,\n    },\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Create a child span within the current job context.\n *\n * Use this for tracing individual tasks or operations within a job.\n * Creates proper parent-child relationships in traces.\n *\n * @param name - Span name (e.g., 'task.validate', 'task.transform')\n * @param fn - Function to execute within the span\n * @param attributes - Optional span attributes\n * @returns Result of the function\n *\n * @example\n * ```ts\n * await runTracedJob('my-job', async (jobSpan) => {\n *   // Create child spans for each task\n *   const result1 = await withChildSpan('task.validate', async (span) => {\n *     span.setAttribute('task.type', 'validation');\n *     return await validateData();\n *   });\n *\n *   const result2 = await withChildSpan('task.transform', async (span) => {\n *     return await transformData(result1);\n *   }, { 'input.size': result1.length });\n *\n *   return { result1, result2 };\n * });\n * ```\n */\nexport async function withChildSpan<T>(\n  name: string,\n  fn: (span: Span) => Promise<T>,\n  attributes?: Attributes,\n): Promise<T> {\n  const tracer = getJobTracer();\n  return await withSpan(tracer, name, fn, {\n    kind: SpanKind.INTERNAL,\n    attributes,\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Add a step event to the current job span.\n *\n * @param stepName - Name of the step\n * @param attributes - Step attributes\n */\nexport function addJobStepEvent(\n  stepName: string,\n  attributes?: Attributes,\n): void {\n  const span = getActiveSpan();\n  if (span) {\n    addSpanEvent(span, `job.step.${stepName}`, attributes);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Record job progress in the current span.\n *\n * @param current - Current progress value\n * @param total - Total expected value\n * @param unit - Unit of measurement (e.g., \"records\", \"items\")\n */\nexport function recordJobProgress(\n  current: number,\n  total: number,\n  unit = 'items',\n): void {\n  const span = getActiveSpan();\n  if (span) {\n    const percentage = Math.round((current / total) * 100);\n    addSpanEvent(span, 'job.progress', {\n      'progress.current': current,\n      'progress.total': total,\n      'progress.percentage': percentage,\n      'progress.unit': unit,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// WORKER METRICS HELPERS\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Record worker metrics in a span.\n *\n * @param span - Span to record metrics in\n * @param metrics - Worker metrics\n */\nexport function recordWorkerMetrics(\n  span: Span,\n  metrics: {\n    activeJobs: number;\n    processedJobs?: number;\n    failedJobs?: number;\n    avgDurationMs?: number;\n  },\n): void {\n  span.setAttributes({\n    [WorkerAttributes.WORKER_ACTIVE_JOBS]: metrics.activeJobs,\n  });\n\n  if (metrics.processedJobs !== undefined) {\n    span.setAttribute('worker.jobs.processed', metrics.processedJobs);\n  }\n  if (metrics.failedJobs !== undefined) {\n    span.setAttribute('worker.jobs.failed', metrics.failedJobs);\n  }\n  if (metrics.avgDurationMs !== undefined) {\n    span.setAttribute('worker.jobs.avg_duration_ms', metrics.avgDurationMs);\n  }\n}\n","numLines":391,"startLine":280,"totalLines":670}}}

#spanLinks: StreamsSpanLinkPort;\n  readonly #metrics: StreamProducerMetrics;\n\n  /** Create stream instrumentation with optional tracer/link adapter overrides. */\n  constructor(options: StreamsInstrumentationOptions = {}) {\n    this.#tracer = options.tracer ?? getTracer('netscript.streams', CORE_PACKAGE_VERSION);\n    this.#spanLinks = options.spanLinks ?? createOtelSdkSpanLink();\n    this.#metrics = new StreamProducerMetrics(options.meter);\n  }\n\n  /** Start a publish span whose context and lifetime survive reconnect. */\n  startPublish(\n    input: Readonly<{\n      streamPath: string;\n      collection: string;\n      operation: string;\n      producerId: string;\n      messageId: string;\n      correlationId?: string;\n    }>,\n  ): StreamsPublishOperation {\n    const span = this.#tracer.startSpan(StreamSpanNames.PUBLISH, {\n      kind: SpanKind.PRODUCER,\n      attributes: streamAttributes({\n        streamPath: input.streamPath,\n        collection: input.collection,\n        operation: input.operation,\n        producerId: input.producerId,\n        messageId: input.messageId,\n        correlationId: input.correlationId,\n      }),\n    });\n    const headers: Record<string, string> = {\n      traceparent: formatTraceparent(span.spanContext()),\n    };\n    const tracestate = span.spanContext().traceState?.serialize();\n    if (tracestate) {\n      headers.tracestate = tracestate;\n    }\n    let ended = false;\n    return {\n      headers,\n      event: (name, attributes) => {\n        if (!ended) {\n          span.addEvent(name, attributes);\n        }\n      },\n      finish: (outcome) => {\n        if (ended) {\n          return;\n        }\n        ended = true;\n        const reason = 'reason' in outcome ? outcome.reason : undefined;\n        span.setAttribute(StreamAttributes.OUTCOME, outcome.status);\n        if ('attempts' in outcome) {\n          span.setAttribute(StreamAttributes.PRODUCER_ATTEMPT, outcome.attempts);\n        }\n        if (reason) {\n          span.setAttribute(StreamAttributes.PRODUCER_REASON, reason);\n        }\n        span.addEvent('stream.producer.settled', {\n          [StreamAttributes.OUTCOME]: outcome.status,\n          ...(reason ? { [StreamAttributes.PRODUCER_REASON]: reason } : {}),\n        });\n        this.#metrics.recordOutcome(input.streamPath, input.producerId, outcome);\n        if (outcome.status === 'delivered') {\n          span.setStatus({ code: SpanStatusCode.OK });\n        } else {\n          span.setStatus({ code: SpanStatusCode.ERROR, message: reason ?? outcome.status });\n        }\n        span.end();\n      },\n    };\n  }\n\n  /** Publish one stream event and finish its span immediately after emission. */\n  publish(\n    input: Readonly<{\n      streamPath: string;\n      collection: string;\n      operation: string;\n      producerId: string;\n      messageId: string;\n      correlationId?: string;\n      emit: (headers: Record<string, string>) => void;\n    }>,\n  ): void {\n    const operation = this.startPublish(input);\n    try {\n      input.emit({ ...operation.headers });\n      operation.finish({ status: 'delivered', attempts: 1 });\n    } catch (error) {\n      operation.finish({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'serialization-failed' });\n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** Record a producer lifecycle transition. */\n  recordState(\n    streamPath: string,\n    producerId: string,\n    snapshot: StreamProducerStateSnapshotV1,\n  ): void {\n    this.#metrics.recordState(streamPath, producerId, snapshot);\n  }\n\n  /** Update observable buffered event and byte values. */\n  recordBuffer(streamPath: string, producerId: string, events: number, bytes: number): void {\n    this.#metrics.recordBuffer(streamPath, producerId, events, bytes);\n  }\n\n  /** Record one bounded retry attempt. */\n  recordRetry(\n    streamPath: string,\n    producerId: string,\n    phase: 'connect' | 'append' | 'close',\n    attempt: number,\n    reason: string,\n  ): void {\n    this.#metrics.recordRetry(streamPath, producerId, phase, attempt, reason);\n  }\n\n  /** Record successful recovery after at least one retry. */\n  recordRecovery(\n    streamPath: string,\n    producerId: string,\n    phase: 'connect' | 'append' | 'close',\n /** Start a CONSUMER span for a stream subscription with fan-in links. */\n  startSubscribeSpan(\n    input: Readonly<{\n      streamPath: string;\n      collection?: string;\n      operation?: 'subscribe',\n      }),\n      links: createFanInLinks(\n        input.messages.map((message) => ({\n          traceparent: message.traceparent,\n          tracestate: message.tracestate,\n          attributes: streamAttributes({\n            streamPath: message.streamPath ?? input.streamPath,\n            collection: message.collection ?? input.collection,\n            operation: message.operation ?? input.operation ?? 'consume',\n            messageId: message.messageId,\n            correlationId: message.correlationId,\n          }),\n        })),\n        this.#spanLinks,\n      ),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n/** Create real stream instrumentation. */\nexport function createStreamsInstrumentation(\n  options: StreamsInstrumentationOptions = {},\n): StreamsInstrumentation {\n  return new StreamsInstrumentation(options);\n}\n\n/** Minimal instrumentation contract understood by NetScript telemetry hosts. */\nexport interface StreamsInstrumentationRegistration {\n  /** Stable instrumentation name. */\n  readonly name: string;\n  /** Register instrumentation hooks with the host telemetry context. */\n  setup(context: unknown): void;\n}\n\n/**\n * Telemetry registration for stream publish, consume, and subscribe spans.\n */\nexport const streamsInstrumentation: StreamsInstrumentationRegistration = {\n  name: 'netscript.streams',\n  setup: (_context) => {\n    void StreamSpanNames;\n    void StreamAttributes;\n  },\n};\n\nfunction streamAttributes(\n  input: Readonly<{\n    streamPath: string;\n    collection?: string;\n    operation?: string;\n    producerId?: string;\n    messageId?: string;\n    correlationId?: string;\n  }>,\n): StreamsTelemetryAttributes {\n  return {\n    [StreamAttributes.SYSTEM]: 'netscript.streams',\n    [StreamAttributes.DESTINATION_NAME]: input.streamPath,\n    [StreamAttributes.DESTINATION_KIND]: 'stream',\n    [StreamAttributes.OPERATION_NAME]: input.operation,\n    [StreamAttributes.OPERATION_TYPE]: input.operation,\n    [StreamAttributes.STREAM_PATH]: input.streamPath,\n    [StreamAttributes.COLLECTION]: input.collection,\n    [StreamAttributes.PRODUCER_ID]: input.producerId,\n    [StreamAttributes.MESSAGE_ID]: input.messageId,\n    [StreamAttributes.MESSAGE_CONVERSATION_ID]: input.correlationId,\n    [StreamAttributes.CORRELATION_ID]: input.correlationId,\n  };\n}\n","numLines":235,"startLine":130,"totalLines":364}}}

DEFAULT_PRODUCER_ID,\n  });\n  return Object.freeze({\n    delete(entity: keyof WorkerStreamEntities, id: string): void {\n      producer.delete(entity, id);\n    },\n    upsert<TEntity extends keyof WorkerStreamEntities>(\n      entity: TEntity,\n      value: WorkerStreamEntities[TEntity],\n    ): void {\n      producer.upsert(entity, { ...value });\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n/** Convert an execution record into the durable stream execution entity shape. */\nexport function toExecutionStreamEntity(execution: WorkerExecutionRecord): WorkerExecution {\n  return {\n    id: execution.id,\n    jobId: execution.jobId,\n    topic: execution.topic ?? The previous `& Record<string, unknown>` widened every known\n// field back to `unknown` whenever a handler spread the record\n// (`{ ...exec, executionId: exec.id }`), which silently erased the precise\n// `status`/`triggeredBy`/`topic`/… types and broke conformance with the\n// `ExecutionRecordResponse` contract. A fixed, fully-enumerated shape keeps\n// spreads precise so the connector handlers type-check against the contract.\nexport type ExecutionRecord = Readonly<\n  {\n    /** Unique execution identifier. */\n    readonly id: string;\n    /** Runtime concept represented by this execution. */\n    readonly concept: ExecutionConcept;\n    /** Job or task identifier associated with the execution. */\n    readonly jobId: string;\n    /** Stream topic associated with the execution. */\n    readonly topic: string;\n    /** Current execution status. */\n    readonly status: ExecutionStatus;\n    /** Source that triggered the execution. */\n    readonly triggeredBy: ExecutionTriggerType;\n    /** ISO timestamp for when the execution was triggered. */\n    readonly triggeredAt: string;\n    /** ISO timestamp for when the execution started. */\n    readonly startedAt: string | null;\n    /** ISO timestamp for when the execution completed. */\n    readonly completedAt: string | null;\n    /** Process-style exit code for the execution result. */\n    readonly exitCode: number | null;\n    /** Execution duration in milliseconds. */\n    readonly duration: number | null;\n    /** Error message recorded for failed executions. */\n    readonly error: string | null;\n    /** Structured execution result payload. */\n    readonly result: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n    /** Worker identifier that ran the execution. */\n    readonly workerId: string | null;\n    /** Current retry attempt number. */\n    readonly attempt: number;\n    /** Maximum retry attempts allowed for the execution. */\n    readonly maxAttempts: number;\n    /** Structured payload captured for the execution. */\n    readonly payload?: Record<string, unknown>;\n    /** Correlation identifier used to join related executions. */\n    readonly correlationId?: string;\n    /** W3C trace context propagated with the execution. */\n    readonly traceparent?: string;\n    /** W3C trace state propagated with the execution. */\n    readonly tracestate?: string;\n  }\n>;\n\n/** Options for creating a worker execution record. */\nexport type CreateExecutionOptions = Readonly<{\n  /** Runtime concept represented by this execution. */\n  concept?: ExecutionConcept;\n  /** Job or task identifier associated with the execution. */\n  jobId: string;\n  /** Stream topic associated with the execution. */\n  topic?: string;\n  /** Source that triggered the execution. */\n  triggeredBy: ExecutionTriggerType;\n  /** Structured payload associated with the execution. */\n  payload?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  /** Correlation identifier used to join related executions. */\n  correlationId?: string;\n  /** Maximum retry attempts allowed for the execution. */\n  maxAttempts?: number;\n  /** W3C trace context parent value. */\n  traceparent?: string;\n  /** W3C trace context state value. */\n  tracestate?: string;\n}>;\n\n/** Options for listing worker execution records. */\nexport type ListExecutionOptions = Readonly<{\n  /** Optional concept filter. */\n  concept?: ExecutionConcept;\n  /** Maximum number of records to return. */\n  limit?: number;\n}>;\n\n/** Options for completing a worker execution record. */\nexport type CompleteExecutionOptions = Readonly<{\n  /** Terminal status assigned to the execution. */\n  status: 'cancelled' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'timeout';\n  /** Process-style exit code for the execution result. */\n  exitCode?: number;\n  /** Error message recorded for failed executions. */\n  error?: string;\n  /** Structured execution result payload. */\n  result?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}>;\n\n/** Hook invoked after execution state mutations. */\nexport type ExecutionMutationHook = (\n  event: Readonly<{\n    /** Mutation type that occurred. */\n    type: 'created' | 'deleted' | 'updated';\n    /** Execution record affected by the mutation. */\n    execution: ExecutionRecord;\n  }>,\n) => void;\n\n/** KV-backed execution state store with explicit construction. */\nexport class KvExecutionState {\n  /** Stable execution state store identifier. */\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly #kv: RegistryKvStore;\n  readonly #workerId: string;\n  #onMutation?: ExecutionMutationHook;\n\n  /** Creates a KV-backed execution state store. */\n  constructor(options: Readonly<{ id?: string; kv: RegistryKvStore; workerId?: string }>) {\n    this.id = options.id ?? 'kv-execution-state';\n    this.#kv = options.kv;\n    this.#workerId = options.workerId ?? crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n\n  /** Registers a callback for execution state mutations. */\n  setMutationHook(hook: ExecutionMutationHook): void {\n    this.#onMutation = hook;\n  }\n\n  /** Creates and persists a pending execution record. */\n  async create(options: CreateExecutionOptions): Promise<ExecutionRecord> {\n    const now = new Date().toISOString();\n    const record = validateExecution({\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      concept: options.concept ?? 'job',\n      jobId: options.jobId,\n      topic: options.topic ?? DEFAULT_TOPIC,\n      status: 'pending',\n      triggeredBy: options.triggeredBy,\n      triggeredAt: now,\n      startedAt: null,\n      completedAt: null,\n      exitCode: null,\n      duration: null,\n      error: null,\n      result: null,\n      workerId: null,\n      attempt: 0,\n      maxAttempts: options.maxAttempts ?? 3,\n      payload: options.payload,\n      correlationId: options.correlationId,\n      traceparent: options.traceparent,\n      tracestate: options.tracestate,\n    });\n    await this.#save(record, 'created');\n    return record;\n  }\n\n  /** Marks an execution as queued. */\n  queue(executionId: string): Promise<ExecutionRecord | null> {\n    return this.#transition(executionId, { status: 'queued' });\n  }\n\n  /** Marks an execution as running and assigns the current worker id. */\n  start(executionId: string): Promise<ExecutionRecord | null> {\n    return this.#transition(executionId, {\n      status: 'running',\n      startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      workerId: this.#workerId,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Marks an execution with a terminal status and completion details. */\n  async complete(\n    executionId: string,\n    options: CompleteExecutionOptions,\n  ): Promise<ExecutionRecord | null> {\n    const current = await this.get(executionId);\n    if (!current) return null;\n    const now = new Date();\n    const startedAt = current.startedAt ? new Date(current.startedAt) : now;\n    return await this.#transition(executionId, {\n      status: options.status,\n      completedAt: now.toISOString(),\n      duration: Math.max(0, now.getTime() - startedAt.getTime()),\n      exitCode: options.exitCode ?? (options.status === 'completed' ? 0 : 1),\n      error: options.error ?? null,\n      result: options.result ?? null,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Reads an execution by id. */\n  async get(executionId: string): Promise<ExecutionRecord | null> {\n    const entry = await this.#kv.get<ExecutionRecord>([...EXECUTION_PREFIX, executionId]);\n    return entry?.value ?? null;\n  }\n\n  /** Lists all executions, optionally filtered by concept. */\n  listAll(options: ListExecutionOptions = {}): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    return this.#list((record) => !options.concept || record.concept === options.concept, options);\n  }\n\n  /** Lists executions for a specific job or task id. */\n  listByJob(jobId: string, options: ListExecutionOptions = {}): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    return this.#list(\n      (record) =>\n        record.jobId === jobId && (!options.concept || record.concept === options.concept),\n      options,\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** Lists executions for a specific topic. */\n  listByTopic(topic: string, options: ListExecutionOptions = {}): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    return this.#list(\n      (record) =>\n        record.topic === topic && (!options.concept || record.concept === options.concept),\n      options,\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** Lists executions with a specific status. */\n  listByStatus(\n    status: ExecutionStatus,\n    options: ListExecutionOptions = {},\n  ): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    return this.#list(\n      (record) =>\n        record.status === status && (!options.concept || record.concept === options.concept),\n      options,\n    );\n  }\n\n  /** Lists executions with a specific correlation id. */\n  listByCorrelation(\n    correlationId: string,\n    options: ListExecutionOptions = {},\n  ): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    return this.#list((record) => record.correlationId === correlationId, options);\n  }\n\n  /** Counts all executions, optionally filtered by concept. */\n  async countAll(concept?: ExecutionConcept): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.listAll({ concept })).length;\n  }\n\n  /** Counts executions for a specific job or task id. */\n  async countByJob(jobId: string, concept?: ExecutionConcept): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.listByJob(jobId, { concept })).length;\n  }\n\n  /** Counts executions for a specific topic. */\n  async countByTopic(topic: string, concept?: ExecutionConcept): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.listByTopic(topic, { concept })).length;\n  }\n\n  /** Counts executions with a specific status. */\n  async countByStatus(status: ExecutionStatus, concept?: ExecutionConcept): Promise<number> {\n    return (await this.listByStatus(status, { concept })).length;\n  }\n\n  /** Deletes an execution by id. */\n  async delete(executionId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n    const current = await this.get(executionId);\n    if (!current) return false;\n    await this.#kv.delete([...EXECUTION_PREFIX, executionId]);\n    this.#onMutation?.({ type: 'deleted', execution: current });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  /** Applies partial execution updates through validation and persistence. */\n  async #transition(\n    executionId: string,\n    updates: Partial<ExecutionRecord>,\n  ): Promise<ExecutionRecord | null> {\n    const current = await this.get(executionId);\n    if (!current) return null;\n    const updated = validateExecution({ ...current, ...updates });\n    await this.#save(updated, 'updated');\n    return updated;\n  }\n\n  /** Persists an execution record and emits the mutation hook. */\n  async #save(record: ExecutionRecord, type: 'created' | 'updated'): Promise<void> {\n    await this.#kv.set([...EXECUTION_PREFIX, record.id], record);\n    this.#onMutation?.({ type, execution: record });\n  }\n\n  /** Lists persisted executions matching a predicate. */\n  async #list(\n    predicate: (record: ExecutionRecord) => boolean,\n    options: ListExecutionOptions,\n  ): Promise<ExecutionRecord[]> {\n    const result: ExecutionRecord[] = [];\n    for await (const entry of this.#kv.list<ExecutionRecord>({ prefix: EXECUTION_PREFIX })) {\n      if (entry.value && predicate(entry.value)) result.push(entry.value);\n    }\n    result.sort((a, b) => b.triggeredAt.localeCompare(a.triggeredAt));\n    return options.limit ? result.slice(0, options.limit) : result;","numLines":320,"startLine":0,"totalLines":328}}}

StreamWriteRejectionReasonV1,\n} from '../domain/producer-contract-v1.ts';\nimport type { StateSchema, StreamStateDefinition } from '../domain/stream-schema.ts';\nimport type { StreamProducerClockPort } from '../ports/stream-producer-clock-port.ts';\nimport type { StreamProducerPort, StreamWriteContextV1 } from '../ports/stream-producer-port.ts';\nimport type { StreamProducerRandomPort } from '../ports/stream-producer-random-port.ts';\nimport type { StreamProducerTransportPort } from '../ports/stream-producer-transport-port.ts';\nimport { DurableStreamProducerTransport } from '../adapters/durable-stream-producer-transport.ts';\nimport { SystemStreamProducerClock } from '../adapters/system-stream-producer-clock.ts';\nimport { SystemStreamProducerRandom } from '../adapters/system-stream-producer-random.ts';\nimport { DurableStreamProducerSupervisor } from './durable-stream-producer-supervisor.ts';\nimport {\n  createStreamsInstrumentation,\n  type StreamsInstrumentation,\n  type StreamsPublishOperation,\n} from '../telemetry/instrumentation.ts';\nimport { buildStreamUrl, getStreamsAuth } from './stream-url-resolver.ts';\n\nexport type { StateSchema, StreamStateDefinition } from '../domain/stream-schema.ts';\nexport type { StreamProducerPort, StreamWriteContextV1 } from '../ports/stream-producer-port.ts';\n\n/** Options accepted by {@link DurableStreamProducer}. */\nexport interface DurableStreamProducerOptions<TDef extends StreamStateDefinition> {\n  /** Stream path relative to the base URL, for example `/workers/executions`. */\n  readonly streamPath: string;\n  /** State schema produced by {@link defineStreamSchema}. */\n  readonly schema: StateSchema<TDef>;\n  /** Stable producer identity for idempotent delivery. */\n  readonly producerId: string;\n  /** Optional finite reconnect policy overrides. */\n  readonly reconnectPolicy?: Partial<StreamProducerReconnectPolicyV1>;\n  /** Optional dual buffer policy overrides. */\n  readonly bufferPolicy?: Partial<StreamProducerBufferPolicyV1>;\n  /** Optional stream instrumentation; defaults to real NetScript telemetry. */\n  readonly instrumentation?: StreamsInstrumentation;\n  /** Optional protocol transport adapter. */\n  readonly transport?: StreamProducerTransportPort;\n  /** Optional backoff clock adapter. */\n  readonly clock?: StreamProducerClockPort;\n  /** Optional reconnect jitter adapter. */\n  readonly random?: StreamProducerRandomPort;\n  /** Optional lifecycle abort signal. */\n  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;\n}\n\n/** Server-side writer for a named durable stream. */\nexport class DurableStreamProducer<TDef extends StreamStateDefinition>\n  implements StreamProducerPort {\n  /** Stream path owned by this producer. */\n  readonly streamPath: string;\n  /** Stable fingerprint used to reject incompatible singleton reuse. */\n  readonly configurationFingerprint: string;\n\n  readonly #schema: StateSchema<TDef>;\n  readonly #producerId: string;\n  readonly #instrumentation: StreamsInstrumentation;\n  readonly #supervisor: DurableStreamProducerSupervisor;\n\n  /** Create a producer and begin connecting under the finite retry policy. */\n  constructor(options: DurableStreamProducerOptions<TDef>) {\n    this.streamPath = options.streamPath;\n    this.configurationFingerprint = producerFingerprint(options);\n    this.#schema = options.schema;\n    this.#producerId = options.producerId;\n    this.#instrumentation = options.instrumentation ?? createStreamsInstrumentation();\n    this.#supervisor = new DurableStreamProducerSupervisor({\n      url: resolveRequiredStreamUrl(this.streamPath),\n      headers: getStreamsAuth(),\n      producerId: options.producerId,\n      transport: options.transport ?? new DurableStreamProducerTransport(),\n      clock: options.clock ?? new SystemStreamProducerClock(),\n      random: options.random ?? new SystemStreamProducerRandom(),\n      reconnectPolicy: options.reconnectPolicy,\n      bufferPolicy: options.bufferPolicy,\n      signal: options.signal,\n      onStopped: () => removeProducer(this.streamPath, this),\n      onState: (snapshot) =>\n        this.#instrumentation.recordState(this.streamPath, this.#producerId, snapshot),\n      onBuffer: (events, bytes) =>\n        this.#instrumentation.recordBuffer(this.streamPath, this.#producerId, events, bytes),\n      onRetry: (phase, attempt, reason) =>\n        this.#instrumentation.recordRetry(\n          this.streamPath,\n          this.#producerId,\n          phase,\n          attempt,\n          reason,\n        ),\n      onRecovery: (phase, attempt) =>\n        this.#instrumentation.recordRecovery(\n          this.streamPath,\n          this.#producerId,\n          phase,\n          attempt,\n        ),\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Current lifecycle and buffer snapshot. */\n  get state(): StreamProducerStateSnapshotV1 {\n    return this.#supervisor.state;\n  }\n\n  /** Whether writes can currently be attempted without reconnecting first. */\n  get isReady(): boolean {\n    return this.#supervisor.isReady;\n  }\n\n  /** Whether shutdown or terminal failure prevents new writes. */\n  get closed(): boolean {\n    return this.#supervisor.closed;\n  }\n\n  /** Resolve on the current or next ready transition. */\n  waitUntilReady(options: StreamProducerReadinessOptionsV1 = {}): Promise<void> {\n    return this.#supervisor.waitUntilReady(options);\n  }\n\n  /** Upsert an entity into a stream collection. */\n  upsert<K extends keyof TDef & string>(\n    entityType: K,\n    value: Record<string, unknown>,\n    context?: StreamWriteContextV1,\n  ): StreamWriteReceiptV1 {\n    if (this.closed) {\n      return this.#reject(stateRejection(this.state.state));\n    }\n    const definition = this.#schema[entityType];\n    if (!definition) {\n      return this.#reject('invalid-entity');\n    }\n    const rawKey = value[definition.primaryKey];\n    const key = rawKey === undefined || rawKey === null ? '' : String(rawKey);\n    if (key === '') {\n      return this.#reject('invalid-key');\n    }\n    return this.#serializeAndEnqueue({\n      entityType,\n      operation: 'upsert',\n      key,\n      value,\n      context,\n      type: definition.type ?? entityType,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Delete an entity from a stream collection by primary key. */\n  delete<K extends keyof TDef & string>(\n    entityType: K,\n    key: string,\n    context?: StreamWriteContextV1,\n  ): StreamWriteReceiptV1 {\n    if (this.closed) {\n      return this.#reject(stateRejection(this.state.state));\n    }\n    const definition = this.#schema[entityType];\n    if (!definition) {\n      return this.#reject('invalid-entity');\n    }\n    if (key === '') {\n      return this.#reject('invalid-key');\n    }\n    return this.#serializeAndEnqueue({\n      entityType,\n      operation: 'delete',\n      key,\n      context,\n      type: definition.type ?? entityType,\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** Wait for writes accepted before this call. */\n  flush(): Promise<void> {\n    return this.#supervisor.flush();\n  }\n\n  /** Stop local work without sending durable EOF. */\n  stop(): Promise<void> {\n    return this.#supervisor.stop();\n  }\n\n  /** Drain accepted writes, acknowledge durable EOF, and stop. */\n  close(): Promise<void> {\n    return this.#supervisor.close();\n  }\n\n  #serializeAndEnqueue(\n    input: Readonly<{\n      entityType: string;\n      operation: 'upsert' | 'delete';\n      type: string;\n      key: string;\n      value?: Record<string, unknown>;\n      context?: StreamWriteContextV1;\n    }>,\n  ): StreamWriteReceiptV1 {\n    const publish = this.#startPublish(\n      input.entityType,\n      input.operation,\n      input.key,\n      input.context,\n    );\n    try {\n      const event = JSON.stringify({\n        type: input.type,\n        key: input.key,\n        ...(input.value ? { value: input.value } : {}),\n        headers: publish.headers,\n      });\n      return this.#supervisor.enqueue(event, publish);\n    } catch {\n      return this.#supervisor.reject('serialization-failed', publish);\n    }\n  }\n\n  #startPublish(\n    entityType: string,\n    operation: 'upsert' | 'delete',\n    key: string,\n    context?: StreamWriteContextV1,\n  ): StreamsPublishOperation {\n    const correlationId = context?.correlationId?.trim() || key;\n    const messageId = context?.messageId?.trim() || key;\n    const publish = this.#instrumentation.startPublish({\n      streamPath: this.streamPath,\n      collection: entityType,\n      operation,\n      producerId: this.#producerId,\n      messageId,\n      correlationId,\n    });\n    return {\n      headers: { operation, correlationId, ...publish.headers },\n      event: (name, attributes) => publish.event(name, attributes),\n      finish: (outcome) => publish.finish(outcome),\n    };\n  }\n\n  #reject(reason: StreamWriteRejectionReasonV1): StreamWriteReceiptV1 {\n    this.#instrumentation.recordRejected(this.streamPath, this.#producerId, reason);\n    return this.#supervisor.reject(reason);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction stateRejection(\n  state: StreamProducerStateSnapshotV1['state'],\n): StreamWriteRejectionReasonV1 {\n  if (state === 'stopping') {\n    return 'producer-stopping';\n  }\n  if (state === 'stopped') {\n    return 'producer-stopped';\n  }\n  return 'producer-failed';\n}\n\nfunction resolveRequiredStreamUrl(streamPath: string): string {\n  try {\n    return buildStreamUrl(streamPath);\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    throw new Error(\n      `[DurableStreamProducer] Missing plugin reference \"streams\" for stream \"${streamPath}\". ` +\n        'Install the streams plugin, then run `netscript service generate` to regenerate Aspire wiring. ' +\n        message,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nfunction producerFingerprint<TDef extends StreamStateDefinition>(\n  options: DurableStreamProducerOptions<TDef>,\n): string {\n  return JSON.stringify({\n    producerId: options.producerId,\n    reconnectPolicy: options.reconnectPolicy ?? {},\n    bufferPolicy: options.bufferPolicy ?? {},\n  });\n}\n\nconst producers = new Map<string, DurableStreamProducer<StreamStateDefinition>>();\n\nfunction removeProducer(\n  streamPath: string,\n  producer: DurableStreamProducer<StreamStateDefinition>,\n): void {\n  if (producers.get(streamPath) === producer) {\n    producers.delete(streamPath);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Create or reuse a compatible durable stream producer for one stream path. */\nexport function createDurableStream<TDef extends StreamStateDefinition>(\n  options: DurableStreamProducerOptions<TDef>,\n): DurableStreamProducer<TDef> {\n  const existing = producers.get(options.streamPath);\n  if (existing && !existing.closed) {\n    const fingerprint = producerFingerprint(options);\n    if (existing.configurationFingerprint !== fingerprint) {\n      throw new Error(`Incompatible durable stream producer options for \"${options.streamPath}\"`);\n    }\n    return existing as DurableStreamProducer<TDef>;\n  }\n  if (existing?.closed) {\n    producers.delete(options.streamPath);\n  }\n\n  const producer = new DurableStreamProducer(options);\n  producers.set(options.streamPath, producer as DurableStreamProducer<StreamStateDefinition>);\n  return producer;\n}\n","numLines":317,"startLine":0,"totalLines":317}}}

get isReady(): boolean {\n    return this.#state.state === 'ready';\n  }\n\n  /** Whether new writes are rejected because shutdown or terminal failure began. */\n  get closed(): boolean {\n    return !this.#accepted;\n  }\n\n  /** Accept an already serialized event into the bounded FIFO. */\n  enqueue(body: string, lifecycle?: StreamProducerWriteLifecycle): StreamWriteReceiptV1 {\n    const rejection = this.#writeRejectionReason();\n    if (rejection) {\n      return this.#queue.reject(rejection, lifecycle);\n    }\n    const receipt = this.#queue.enqueue(body, lifecycle);\n    this.#kick();\n    return receipt;\n  }\n\n  /** Return an already-settled receipt for a write rejected before queue admission. */\n  reject(\n    reason: StreamWriteRejectionReasonV1,\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1786521176-5frlv0NAPXrGAdIqrL2t","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"text","text":"So the publish path is asynchronous (via the supervisor queue). Even if D3 wraps the publish call in a `context.with(...)`, **the actual span is started inside `startPublish` which captures the OTel context at the moment of the call** — but the **transport-level delivery** happens asynchronously later. Let me re-read more carefully.\n\nActually, the publish span (`this.#instrumentation.startPublish(...)`) starts synchronously when the producer's `upsert` is called. If D3 wraps that call with `context.with(extractedCtx, () => producer.upsert(...))`, then `context.active()` during `startSpan` will be the extracted context. {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1786521185-5KQ0460zG9onVSt7z14M","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"text","text":"So `getTracer('netscript.streams', ...)` returns `trace.getTracer(name, version)` from `@opentelemetry/api`. When `startSpan` is called without an explicit parent context in the `StreamsTracerPort`, **the resulting span will be rooted at the current OTel context**.\n\nNow the plan's D3 says \"publish under an OTel context extracted from the execution record's stored `traceparent`/`tracestate`\". Let me verify whether this is actually feasible by checking if the `StreamsInstrumentation.startPublish` accepts a parent context — and it does not. The implementation would need to wrap the `upsert` call with `context.with(extracted, () => producer.upsert(...))`. But the producer is a singleton module-level state. Now let me look at what `startSpan` on a real OTel tracer does in the absence of an explicit parent context:","citations":[]}],"model":"minimax/minimax-m3","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"output_tokens_details":null,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"Novita","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6bc63180-9263-4b3d-b5b5-34a4f46e2eb7","uuid":"45ca5bf6-4715-4993-bfdf-920dabf49168","timestamp":"2026-08-12T07:53:12.067Z"}

The Deno-native provider\n22\t * does not yet preserve per-link attributes, which the descriptor advertises.\n23\t */\n24\texport const otelDenoDescriptor: TelemetryProviderDescriptor = {\n25\t  id: 'otel-deno',\n26\t  description: \"Deno runtime's auto-registered global OpenTelemetry provider.\",\n27\t  supportsLinkAttributes: false,\n28\t};\n29\t\n30\t/**\n31\t * Provider adapter that binds to Deno's auto-registered global OpenTelemetry\n32\t * provider.\n33\t *\n34\t * This is the default provider for NetScript telemetry. Because the Deno\n35\t * runtime registers the global provider itself when `OTEL_DENO=true`,\n36\t * {@linkcode OtelDenoTracerProvider.register} is a documented no-op; user code\n37\t * must not call `setGlobalTracerProvider` on top of it.\n38\t */\n39\texport class OtelDenoTracerProvider implements TracerProviderPort {\n40\t  /** Capability descriptor advertising attribute-less links. */\n41\t  readonly descriptor: TelemetryProviderDescriptor = otelDenoDescriptor;\n42\t\n43\t  /**\n44\t   * No-op: the Deno runtime auto-registers the global provider when\n45\t   * `OTEL_DENO=true`. Present to satisfy {@linkcode TracerProviderPort}.\n46\t   */\n47\t  register(): void {\n48\t    // Intentionally empty; Deno owns global provider registration.\n49\t  }\n50\t\n51\t  /**\n52\t   * Report whether a non-noop tracer provider is registered in this process.\n53\t   *\n54\t   * Returns `false` when telemetry is disabled (Deno leaves the built-in\n55\t   * no-op provider in place) and `true` once the runtime has installed its\n56\t   * OTLP-backed provider.\n57\t   */\n58\t  isActive(): boolean {\n59\t    const provider = trace.getTracerProvider();\n60\t    return provider?.constructor?.name !== 'NoopTracerProvider';\n61\t  }\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\t/**\n65\t * Propagator adapter delegating to the global OpenTelemetry propagator that the\n66\t * Deno runtime registers.\n67\t *\n68\t * Inject/extract flow through `@opentelemetry/api`'s global propagation API, so\n69\t * the active W3C Trace Context propagator (installed by the runtime) does the\n70\t * work; when telemetry is off the global propagator is a documented no-op.\n71\t */\n72\texport class OtelDenoPropagator implements PropagatorPort {\n73\t  /** Inject the current trace context into `carrier` as W3C headers. */\n74\t  inject(context: Context, carrier: PropagationInjectCarrier): void {\n75\t    propagation.inject(context, carrier);\n76\t  }\n77\t\n78\t  /** Extract a trace context from `carrier`, falling back to `context`. */\n79\t  extract(context: Context, carrier: PropagationExtractCarrier): Context {\n80\t    return propagation.extract(context, carrier);\n81\t  }\n82\t\n83\t  /** Carrier keys the global propagator reads and writes. */\n84\t  fields(): string[] {\n85\t    return propagation.fields();\n86\t  }\n87\t}\n88\t\n89\t/**\n90\t * Meter adapter delegating to the global OpenTelemetry meter that the Deno\n91\t * runtime registers.\n92\t *\n93\t * {@linkcode OtelDenoMeter.forceFlush} is a no-op: the Deno runtime owns the\n94\t * metric export pipeline, so there is nothing for the adapter to drain.\n95\t */\n96\texport class OtelDenoMeter implements MeterPort {\n97\t  /** Obtain a {@linkcode Meter} from the global meter provider. */\n98\t counter = meter.createCounter(counterName, options);\n103\t        return {\n104\t          add: (value: number, attributes?: Attributes) => counter.add(value, attributes),\n105\t        };\n106\t      },\n107\t      createHistogram: (histogramName, options) => {\n108\t        const histogram = meter.createHistogram(histogramName, options);\n109\t        return {\n110\t          record: (value: number, attributes?: Attributes) => histogram.record(value, attributes),\n111\t        };\n112\t      },\n113\t      createObservableGauge: (gaugeName, options) => {\n114\t        const gauge = meter.createObservableGauge(gaugeName, options);\n115\t        return {\n116\t          addCallback: (callback: ObservableCallback) => gauge.addCallback(callback),\n117\t          removeCallback:
